AGENT: planner_agent
----------------------------------------------------------------------
coder_agent: Please execute the following self-contained DISCOVER_DATASET step. It must use only the pinned registry via `mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)`, create/verify the required artifacts, and write required mm records. Do not fabricate data.

STATE_TASK: DISCOVER_DATASET

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json
import pandas as pd
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

WORK_DIR = "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace"
STATE_NOW = "DISCOVER_DATASET"

os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir = Path(WORK_DIR)
profile_path = work_dir / "dataset_profile.json"
head_path = work_dir / "dataset_head.csv"
target_candidates_path = work_dir / "target_candidates.csv"

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

def rag_has(kind, query, dataset_id):
    hits = mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=10,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": dataset_id}
    )
    return bool(hits)

def normalize_candidates(candidates):
    """
    Convert mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns) output into a compact tabular list.
    Handles list[str], list[dict], dict, tuple-like values.
    """
    rows = []

    if candidates is None:
        return rows

    if isinstance(candidates, dict):
        for idx, (k, v) in enumerate(candidates.items(), start=1):
            if isinstance(v, dict):
                row = {"rank": idx, "candidate": k}
                for kk, vv in v.items():
                    if isinstance(vv, (str, int, float, bool)) or vv is None:
                        row[str(kk)] = vv
                    else:
                        row[str(kk)] = json.dumps(vv, default=str)[:500]
                rows.append(row)
            elif isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):
                rows.append({
                    "rank": idx,
                    "candidate": k,
                    "details": json.dumps(v, default=str)[:500]
                })
            else:
                rows.append({
                    "rank": idx,
                    "candidate": k,
                    "details": str(v)[:500]
                })
        return rows

    if isinstance(candidates, (list, tuple)):
        for idx, item in enumerate(candidates, start=1):
            if isinstance(item, dict):
                row = {"rank": idx}
                for kk, vv in item.items():
                    if isinstance(vv, (str, int, float, bool)) or vv is None:
                        row[str(kk)] = vv
                    else:
                        row[str(kk)] = json.dumps(vv, default=str)[:500]
                if "candidate" not in row:
                    # Try common alternatives.
                    for alt in ["column", "name", "target", "field"]:
                        if alt in row:
                            row["candidate"] = row[alt]
                            break
                rows.append(row)
            else:
                rows.append({
                    "rank": idx,
                    "candidate": str(item)
                })
        return rows

    return [{"rank": 1, "candidate": str(candidates)}]

# Check whether state was already completed.
state_hits = mm.rag_search(
    query=f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    kind="pipeline_state",
    k=5,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
state_already_completed = bool(state_hits)

# Verify artifact files.
missing_files = []
if not profile_path.exists():
    missing_files.append(str(profile_path))
if not head_path.exists():
    missing_files.append(str(head_path))
if not target_candidates_path.exists():
    missing_files.append(str(target_candidates_path))

# Verify mm records.
has_dataset_artifact_record = rag_has(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET dataset_profile dataset_head",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)
has_target_candidates_record = rag_has(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET target candidates",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)
has_pipeline_state_record = rag_has(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query=f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)

# If already completed, only repair missing artifacts/records.
# If not completed, create all required artifacts/records.
need_dataset_artifacts = (not profile_path.exists()) or (not head_path.exists()) or (not has_dataset_artifact_record)
need_target_candidates = (not target_candidates_path.exists()) or (not has_target_candidates_record)

if need_dataset_artifacts or need_target_candidates:
    # Read actual pinned dataset. No synthetic fallback allowed.
    df = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False)

    # Required artifact: dataset_head.csv
    if not head_path.exists():
        df.head(20).to_csv(head_path, index=False)

    # Required artifact: dataset_profile.json
    if not profile_path.exists():
        numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
        object_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=["object", "category"]).columns.tolist()
        datetime_like_cols = []

        for col in df.columns:
            col_lower = str(col).lower()
            if any(token in col_lower for token in ["date", "year", "month", "time"]):
                datetime_like_cols.append(col)

        missing_counts = df.isna().sum().sort_values(ascending=False)
        missing_pct = (df.isna().mean() * 100).sort_values(ascending=False)

        sample_values = {}
        for col in df.columns[:100]:
            vals = df[col].dropna().astype(str).unique()[:5].tolist()
            sample_values[col] = vals

        numeric_summary = {}
        if numeric_cols:
            desc = df[numeric_cols].describe().transpose()
            for col, row in desc.iterrows():
                numeric_summary[col] = {
                    "count": float(row.get("count", 0)),
                    "mean": None if pd.isna(row.get("mean")) else float(row.get("mean")),
                    "std": None if pd.isna(row.get("std")) else float(row.get("std")),
                    "min": None if pd.isna(row.get("min")) else float(row.get("min")),
                    "25%": None if pd.isna(row.get("25%")) else float(row.get("25%")),
                    "50%": None if pd.isna(row.get("50%")) else float(row.get("50%")),
                    "75%": None if pd.isna(row.get("75%")) else float(row.get("75%")),
                    "max": None if pd.isna(row.get("max")) else float(row.get("max")),
                }

        profile = {
            "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
            "file_path": FILE_PATH,
            "state": STATE_NOW,
            "created_at_utc": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
            "shape": {
                "rows": int(df.shape[0]),
                "columns": int(df.shape[1]),
            },
            "columns": df.columns.astype(str).tolist(),
            "dtypes": {str(k): str(v) for k, v in df.dtypes.items()},
            "numeric_columns": numeric_cols,
            "categorical_or_text_columns": object_cols,
            "datetime_like_columns_by_name": datetime_like_cols,
            "missing_top_50": [
                {
                    "column": str(col),
                    "missing_count": int(missing_counts.loc[col]),
                    "missing_pct": float(missing_pct.loc[col]),
                }
                for col in missing_counts.index[:50]
            ],
            "sample_values_first_100_columns": sample_values,
            "numeric_summary": numeric_summary,
        }

        with open(profile_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(profile, f, indent=2)

    # Required artifact: target_candidates.csv
    if not target_candidates_path.exists():
        candidates_raw = mm.discover_condition_candidates(df.columns.astype(str).tolist())
        candidate_rows = normalize_candidates(candidates_raw)
        candidate_df = pd.DataFrame(candidate_rows)
        if candidate_df.empty:
            candidate_df = pd.DataFrame(columns=["rank", "candidate", "details"])
        candidate_df.to_csv(target_candidates_path, index=False)

# Load lightweight metadata for records/gate.
with open(profile_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    profile_loaded = json.load(f)

target_candidates_df = pd.read_csv(target_candidates_path)
target_candidate_cols = target_candidates_df.columns.astype(str).tolist()
target_candidate_preview = target_candidates_df.head(20).to_dict(orient="records")

# Add/repair dataset_artifact record.
if not has_dataset_artifact_record:
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="dataset_artifact",
        text=(
            "DISCOVER_DATASET dataset artifacts saved: "
            f"profile={profile_path}, head={head_path}. "
            f"Shape={profile_loaded.get('shape')}; "
            f"columns_count={len(profile_loaded.get('columns', []))}."
        ),
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "artifacts_saved": [str(profile_path), str(head_path)],
            "dataset_profile_path": str(profile_path),
            "dataset_head_path": str(head_path),
            "shape": profile_loaded.get("shape"),
            "columns": profile_loaded.get("columns", []),
        }
    )

# Add/repair target_candidates record.
if not has_target_candidates_record:
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="target_candidates",
        text=(
            "DISCOVER_DATASET target candidates discovered using "
            "mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns). "
            f"Saved to {target_candidates_path}. "
            f"Candidate rows={len(target_candidates_df)}."
        ),
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "artifacts_saved": [str(target_candidates_path)],
            "target_candidates_path": str(target_candidates_path),
            "candidate_columns": target_candidate_cols,
            "candidate_preview": target_candidate_preview,
        }
    )

# Final hard gate verification by files + mm records.
gate_files_ok = profile_path.exists() and head_path.exists() and target_candidates_path.exists()

gate_dataset_artifact_record_ok = rag_has(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET dataset_profile dataset_head",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)
gate_target_candidates_record_ok = rag_has(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET target candidates",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)

# Only write pipeline_state after required artifacts and records exist.
if gate_files_ok and gate_dataset_artifact_record_ok and gate_target_candidates_record_ok and not has_pipeline_state_record:
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="pipeline_state",
        text=(
            f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}. "
            "Required DISCOVER_DATASET artifacts and mm records verified."
        ),
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "artifacts_saved": [
                str(profile_path),
                str(head_path),
                str(target_candidates_path),
            ],
        }
    )

gate_pipeline_state_ok = rag_has(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query=f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    dataset_id=DATASET_ID
)

missing_gate_items = []
if not profile_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(profile_path))
if not head_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(head_path))
if not target_candidates_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(target_candidates_path))
if not gate_dataset_artifact_record_ok:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="dataset_artifact"')
if not gate_target_candidates_record_ok:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="target_candidates"')
if not gate_pipeline_state_ok:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="pipeline_state" with PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET')

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "DISCOVER_DATASET hard gate failed. Missing: "
        + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

print(json.dumps({
    "status": "DISCOVER_DATASET_COMPLETED",
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "artifacts": {
        "dataset_profile": str(profile_path),
        "dataset_head": str(head_path),
        "target_candidates": str(target_candidates_path),
    },
    "shape": profile_loaded.get("shape"),
    "target_candidates_rows": int(len(target_candidates_df)),
    "state_already_completed_before_run": state_already_completed,
}, indent=2))
```

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

import pandas as pd

# Optional plotting dependency; fail only if plot cannot be produced.
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

WORK_DIR = "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace"
STATE_NOW = "DISCOVER_DATASET"

os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)
plots_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "plots"
plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

# Fail-fast governance.
if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir_resolved = Path(WORK_DIR).resolve()
file_path_resolved = Path(FILE_PATH).resolve()

try:
    file_path_resolved.relative_to(work_dir_resolved)
    raise RuntimeError(f"Registry FILE_PATH must not be inside WORK_DIR: {FILE_PATH}")
except ValueError:
    pass

pinned = os.environ.get("PINNED_FILE_PATH")
if pinned and str(Path(pinned).resolve()) != str(file_path_resolved):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "PINNED_FILE_PATH does not match registry latest_path. "
        f"PINNED_FILE_PATH={pinned}; registry latest_path={FILE_PATH}"
    )

profile_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "dataset_profile.json"
head_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "dataset_head.csv"
target_candidates_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_candidates.csv"
missingness_plot_path = plots_dir / "dataset_missingness_top20.png"

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

required_candidate_columns = [
    "column",
    "family",
    "family_confidence",
    "reasons",
    "leakage_guard",
]

def rag_hits(kind, query, k=10):
    return mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=k,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
    )

def has_record(kind, query):
    return bool(rag_hits(kind, query, k=10))

def normalize_target_candidates(candidates):
    """
    Normalize the output of mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns) into the
    reviewer-required exact ordered schema:
      column,family,family_confidence,reasons,leakage_guard

    The candidate content is sourced only from mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns).
    No target candidates are invented. If the mm helper returns no candidate rows,
    this function returns an empty dataframe and the caller fails fast.
    """
    if candidates is None:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=required_candidate_columns)

    if isinstance(candidates, pd.DataFrame):
        raw_df = candidates.copy()
    elif isinstance(candidates, list):
        raw_df = pd.DataFrame(candidates)
    elif isinstance(candidates, tuple):
        raw_df = pd.DataFrame(list(candidates))
    elif isinstance(candidates, dict):
        # Common patterns:
        # 1) {"candidates": [{...}, {...}]}
        # 2) {"IRI_mean_y": {"family": "...", ...}, ...}
        if "candidates" in candidates and isinstance(candidates["candidates"], (list, tuple)):
            raw_df = pd.DataFrame(list(candidates["candidates"]))
        else:
            rows = []
            for key, val in candidates.items():
                if isinstance(val, dict):
                    row = {"column": key}
                    row.update(val)
                    rows.append(row)
                else:
                    rows.append({
                        "column": key,
                        "family": "",
                        "family_confidence": "",
                        "reasons": str(val),
                        "leakage_guard": "",
                    })
            raw_df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    else:
        raw_df = pd.DataFrame([{
            "column": str(candidates),
            "family": "",
            "family_confidence": "",
            "reasons": "Returned as scalar by mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns)",
            "leakage_guard": "",
        }])

    if raw_df.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=required_candidate_columns)

    # Normalize possible synonym fields to exact schema without inventing candidates.
    raw_df.columns = [str(c) for c in raw_df.columns]

    rename_map = {}
    lower_to_original = {str(c).lower(): c for c in raw_df.columns}

    for synonym in ["column", "candidate", "target", "name", "field"]:
        if synonym in lower_to_original:
            rename_map[lower_to_original[synonym]] = "column"
            break

    for synonym in ["family", "condition_family", "target_family", "category"]:
        if synonym in lower_to_original:
            rename_map[lower_to_original[synonym]] = "family"
            break

    for synonym in ["family_confidence", "confidence", "score", "probability"]:
        if synonym in lower_to_original:
            rename_map[lower_to_original[synonym]] = "family_confidence"
            break

    for synonym in ["reasons", "reason", "rationale", "details", "evidence"]:
        if synonym in lower_to_original:
            rename_map[lower_to_original[synonym]] = "reasons"
            break

    for synonym in ["leakage_guard", "leakage", "leakage_note", "leakage_risk", "guard"]:
        if synonym in lower_to_original:
            rename_map[lower_to_original[synonym]] = "leakage_guard"
            break

    df = raw_df.rename(columns=rename_map).copy()

    if "column" not in df.columns:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns) returned candidates but no usable "
            "column/candidate/target/name/field key to populate required 'column' field. "
            f"Returned columns: {list(raw_df.columns)}"
        )

    # Add missing metadata fields as blank/neutral fields; do not add candidate rows.
    for c in required_candidate_columns:
        if c not in df.columns:
            df[c] = ""

    # Convert nested values to short strings for CSV/mm lightweight compatibility.
    for c in required_candidate_columns:
        df[c] = df[c].apply(
            lambda x: json.dumps(x, default=str) if isinstance(x, (list, dict, tuple)) else x
        )

    df = df[required_candidate_columns].copy()
    df["column"] = df["column"].astype(str)

    # Drop rows with blank/null candidate column.
    df = df[df["column"].notna() & (df["column"].astype(str).str.strip() != "")]

    return df

# Check prior completion. Because reviewer requested repair, we may repair invalid/missing artifacts
# but we do not advance beyond DISCOVER_DATASET.
state_hits_before = rag_hits(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query=f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    k=5
)
state_already_completed_before_run = bool(state_hits_before)

# Load the real pinned dataset. No synthetic fallback.
df = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False)
columns = [str(c) for c in df.columns.tolist()]

# Save/repair dataset_head.csv.
df.head(20).to_csv(head_path, index=False)

# Save/repair dataset_profile.json.
numeric_cols = df.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.astype(str).tolist()
categorical_cols = df.select_dtypes(include=["object", "category"]).columns.astype(str).tolist()
datetime_like_cols = [
    str(col) for col in df.columns
    if any(token in str(col).lower() for token in ["date", "year", "month", "time"])
]

missing_counts = df.isna().sum().sort_values(ascending=False)
missing_pct = (df.isna().mean() * 100).sort_values(ascending=False)

sample_values = {}
for col in columns[:100]:
    vals = df[col].dropna().astype(str).unique()[:5].tolist()
    sample_values[col] = vals

numeric_summary = {}
if numeric_cols:
    desc = df[numeric_cols].describe().transpose()
    for col, row in desc.iterrows():
        numeric_summary[str(col)] = {
            "count": None if pd.isna(row.get("count")) else float(row.get("count")),
            "mean": None if pd.isna(row.get("mean")) else float(row.get("mean")),
            "std": None if pd.isna(row.get("std")) else float(row.get("std")),
            "min": None if pd.isna(row.get("min")) else float(row.get("min")),
            "25%": None if pd.isna(row.get("25%")) else float(row.get("25%")),
            "50%": None if pd.isna(row.get("50%")) else float(row.get("50%")),
            "75%": None if pd.isna(row.get("75%")) else float(row.get("75%")),
            "max": None if pd.isna(row.get("max")) else float(row.get("max")),
        }

profile = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "created_at_utc": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
    "shape": {
        "rows": int(df.shape[0]),
        "columns": int(df.shape[1]),
    },
    "columns": columns,
    "dtypes": {str(k): str(v) for k, v in df.dtypes.items()},
    "numeric_columns": numeric_cols,
    "categorical_or_text_columns": categorical_cols,
    "datetime_like_columns_by_name": datetime_like_cols,
    "missing_top_50": [
        {
            "column": str(col),
            "missing_count": int(missing_counts.loc[col]),
            "missing_pct": float(missing_pct.loc[col]),
        }
        for col in missing_counts.index[:50]
    ],
    "sample_values_first_100_columns": sample_values,
    "numeric_summary": numeric_summary,
}
with open(profile_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(profile, f, indent=2)

# Save/repair required diagnostic PNG under WORK_DIR/plots.
top_missing = missing_pct.head(20).sort_values(ascending=True)
plt.figure(figsize=(10, 7))
plt.barh([str(x) for x in top_missing.index], top_missing.values)
plt.xlabel("Missing values (%)")
plt.ylabel("Column")
plt.title("Top 20 Missingness by Column")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(missingness_plot_path, dpi=150)
plt.close()

# Regenerate target_candidates.csv ONLY from mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns).
candidates = mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns)
target_df = normalize_target_candidates(candidates)

if target_df.empty:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "target_candidates.csv would be empty. "
        "mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns) returned no usable candidates."
    )

if list(target_df.columns) != required_candidate_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Internal target candidate schema normalization failed. "
        f"Got {list(target_df.columns)}, expected {required_candidate_columns}"
    )

target_df.to_csv(target_candidates_path, index=False)

# Verify exact saved schema.
saved_target_df = pd.read_csv(target_candidates_path)
if list(saved_target_df.columns) != required_candidate_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Saved target_candidates.csv schema invalid. "
        f"Got {list(saved_target_df.columns)}, expected {required_candidate_columns}"
    )
if len(saved_target_df) < 1:
    raise RuntimeError("Saved target_candidates.csv is empty.")

# Verify files exist before mm logging.
png_files = sorted(str(p) for p in plots_dir.glob("*.png"))
required_files = [
    str(profile_path),
    str(head_path),
    str(target_candidates_path),
]
for p in required_files:
    if not Path(p).exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Required artifact missing after repair: {p}")
if not png_files:
    raise RuntimeError(f"No PNG diagnostic plot found under {plots_dir}")

# Write/repair lightweight mm records.
mm.rag_add(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    text=(
        "DISCOVER_DATASET repaired/verified dataset artifacts. "
        f"dataset_profile.json={profile_path}; dataset_head.csv={head_path}; "
        f"diagnostic_png_count={len(png_files)}; shape={profile['shape']}; "
        f"columns={columns}"
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "artifacts_saved": [str(profile_path), str(head_path)] + png_files,
        "dataset_profile_path": str(profile_path),
        "dataset_head_path": str(head_path),
        "diagnostic_plot_paths": png_files,
        "shape": profile["shape"],
        "columns": columns,
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="target_candidates",
    text=(
        "DISCOVER_DATASET target_candidates.csv regenerated only from "
        "mm.discover_condition_candidates(columns). "
        f"Exact schema={required_candidate_columns}; rows={len(saved_target_df)}; "
        f"path={target_candidates_path}"
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "artifacts_saved": [str(target_candidates_path)],
        "target_candidates_path": str(target_candidates_path),
        "target_candidates_columns": list(saved_target_df.columns),
        "target_candidates_row_count": int(len(saved_target_df)),
        "candidate_columns": saved_target_df["column"].astype(str).tolist(),
    }
)

# Verify mm records exist before final pipeline state record.
dataset_artifact_hits = rag_hits(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET dataset artifacts dataset_profile dataset_head diagnostic",
    k=10
)
target_candidate_hits = rag_hits(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET target_candidates.csv mm.discover_condition_candidates exact schema",
    k=10
)

if not dataset_artifact_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="dataset_artifact" after repair.')
if not target_candidate_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="target_candidates" after repair.')

mm.rag_add(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    text=(
        f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}. "
        "DISCOVER_DATASET hard gate repaired and verified: required files exist, "
        "target_candidates.csv exact schema verified, diagnostic PNG exists, "
        "dataset_artifact and target_candidates mm records exist."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "artifacts_saved": [str(profile_path), str(head_path), str(target_candidates_path)] + png_files,
        "target_candidates_columns": list(saved_target_df.columns),
        "target_candidates_row_count": int(len(saved_target_df)),
    }
)

pipeline_hits = rag_hits(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query=f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    k=10
)

# Final hard gate verification.
missing_gate_items = []
if not profile_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(profile_path))
if not head_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(head_path))
if not target_candidates_path.exists():
    missing_gate_items.append(str(target_candidates_path))
if not png_files:
    missing_gate_items.append(f"at least one PNG under {plots_dir}")
if not dataset_artifact_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="dataset_artifact"')
if not target_candidate_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="target_candidates"')
if not pipeline_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append(
        'mm record kind="pipeline_state" with PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET'
    )

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "DISCOVER_DATASET hard gate failed after repair. Missing: "
        + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

report = {
    "status": "DISCOVER_DATASET_REPAIRED_AND_VERIFIED",
    "DATASET_ID": DATASET_ID,
    "FILE_PATH": FILE_PATH,
    "file_exists": os.path.exists(FILE_PATH),
    "file_path_not_inside_work_dir": True,
    "pinned_file_path_matches_registry": (
        True if not pinned else str(Path(pinned).resolve()) == str(file_path_resolved)
    ),
    "dataset_shape": [int(df.shape[0]), int(df.shape[1])],
    "columns_as_List_str": columns,
    "artifacts": {
        "dataset_profile_json": str(profile_path),
        "dataset_head_csv": str(head_path),
        "target_candidates_csv": str(target_candidates_path),
        "diagnostic_pngs": png_files,
    },
    "target_candidates_csv_columns": list(saved_target_df.columns),
    "target_candidates_csv_row_count": int(len(saved_target_df)),
    "target_candidates_csv_preview": saved_target_df.head(10).to_dict(orient="records"),
    "mm_record_confirmation": {
        "dataset_artifact_hits": len(dataset_artifact_hits),
        "target_candidates_hits": len(target_candidate_hits),
        "pipeline_state_hits": len(pipeline_hits),
        "pipeline_state_completed_text": f"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED={STATE_NOW}",
    },
    "state_already_completed_before_repair": state_already_completed_before_run,
    "state_status_after_repair": "completed_verified_pending_reviewer_approval",
}

print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
```

TARGET_SELECT design from data_scientist_agent:

## Final target recommendation

Select:

```text
IRI_mean_y
```

as the final modeling target.

Treat `IRI_mean_y` as a **continuous regression target**.

Rationale:

- `IRI` / International Roughness Index is a standard pavement condition and ride-quality measure.
- `IRI_mean_y` appears in the approved target candidates with family `iri` and high confidence.
- The `_y` suffix strongly suggests the outcome-side or later-condition variable in a joined before/after or feature/response dataset.
- Predicting future or post-treatment IRI is directly aligned with pavement condition prediction and transportation asset management use cases.

---

## Why competing candidates are not selected

Approved target candidate preview included:

```text
IRI_mean_x
IRI_mean_y
Cracking_Percent_mean_x
Rutting_mean_x
```

### 1. `IRI_mean_x`

Do **not** select `IRI_mean_x` as the target.

Reason:

- The `_x` suffix suggests it is from the predictor/input side of the merged dataset.
- It is more appropriate as a baseline/current-condition feature if it temporally precedes `IRI_mean_y`.
- Selecting `IRI_mean_x` would likely model current IRI rather than future/post-treatment IRI.

Leakage policy:

- `IRI_mean_x` may be used as a feature **only if** it is confirmed to be measured before `IRI_mean_y`.
- It must not be transformed using `IRI_mean_y`.
- Any feature derived from both `IRI_mean_x` and `IRI_mean_y`, such as delta IRI, improvement, deterioration rate, or ratio, must be banned unless used in a separate post hoc analysis and not in prediction.

### 2. `Cracking_Percent_mean_x`

Do **not** select as final target.

Reason:

- Cracking is an important pavement distress measure, but the available column is `_x`, likely an input-side condition variable.
- The current pipeline objective is pavement condition prediction using `IRI_mean_y`.
- It may be retained as an explanatory feature if measured before the target.

### 3. `Rutting_mean_x`

Do **not** select as final target.

Reason:

- Rutting is also an important pavement distress measure, but the available candidate is `_x`.
- It is better treated as a predictor of future ride quality or condition.
- It may be retained as a feature if temporally prior to `IRI_mean_y`.

---

## Regression target strategy

`IRI_mean_y` should be modeled as a numeric continuous target.

Expected modeling task:

```text
Supervised regression
```

Primary target variable:

```text
target_column = "IRI_mean_y"
```

Rows with missing `IRI_mean_y` should be removed from modeling, not imputed as the target.

Recommended target validation in coder step:

- Confirm `IRI_mean_y` exists in dataset columns.
- Confirm it is numeric or safely coercible to numeric.
- Count missing target rows.
- Drop rows with missing target for downstream modeling.
- Save target distribution statistics and target histogram.

---

## Leakage controls for downstream modeling

The target-selection artifact should explicitly define these rules.

### Hard-banned leakage features

Ban from model inputs:

```python
[
  "IRI_mean_y"
]
```

Also ban any future/post-outcome or target-derived columns if they appear later, including columns matching patterns such as:

```text
*_y
target
label
outcome
post
future_condition
delta_iri
iri_change
improvement
deterioration
```

However, this dataset includes `Future_AADT_mean_x`, which is a traffic forecast variable rather than a target-side pavement condition. It should be reviewed during feature engineering, not automatically banned only because it contains `Future`.

### Special handling: `IRI_mean_x`

`IRI_mean_x` should be classified as a **potential baseline condition feature**, not as the target.

Recommended leakage status:

```text
conditionally_allowed_feature
```

Condition:

- Allowed only if interpreted as pre-treatment/current/baseline IRI measured before the outcome `IRI_mean_y`.

If temporal provenance cannot be established in later EDA/modeling, run two model sets:

1. **Full baseline model** with `IRI_mean_x`
2. **Strict no-prior-IRI model** excluding `IRI_mean_x`

This will quantify dependence on prior IRI and protect against hidden leakage.

### `_y` suffix controls

For this dataset, downstream features should generally exclude all non-target `_y` columns if present.

Current approved columns show only:

```text
IRI_mean_y
```

as a `_y` column. It is the target and must be removed from `X`.

---

    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
```

The model metric searches may return no hits at this stage; that is acceptable and should be logged as “not yet available,” not treated as failure.

---

## Coder execution guidance for TARGET_SELECT

The coder step should:

1. Use only pinned registry:

   ```python
   reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
   FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
   DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]
   ```

2. Check prior state completion:

   ```python
   mm.rag_search(
       query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
       kind="pipeline_state",
       k=5,
       meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
   )
   ```

3. Use approved discovery artifact:

   ```text
   /content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_candidates.csv
   ```

4. Confirm exact candidate schema:

   ```text
   column,family,family_confidence,reasons,leakage_guard
   ```

5. Confirm `IRI_mean_y` exists in candidate list and dataset columns.

6. Confirm `IRI_mean_y` is numeric or coercible to numeric.

7. Save required artifacts.

8. Write required mm records.

9. Print gate evidence:
   - `DATASET_ID`
   - `FILE_PATH`
   - selected target
   - task type
   - candidate file path
   - candidate schema
   - artifact paths
   - target diagnostics summary
   - leakage policy summary
   - mm record confirmations

Final target-selection decision:

```text
Selected target: IRI_mean_y
Task type: regression
Status: ready for coder_agent TARGET_SELECT artifact creation and reviewer approval
```

----------------------------------------------------------------------
AGENT: coder_agent
----------------------------------------------------------------------
Acknowledged. Awaiting planner routing for coder_agent execution of `TARGET_SELECT`.

I will not execute until the planner explicitly instructs coder_agent with the TARGET_SELECT code/request.

----------------------------------------------------------------------
AGENT: reviewer_agent
----------------------------------------------------------------------
STATUS: REJECT

SUMMARY:
- TARGET_SELECT cannot be reviewed or approved because coder_agent has not yet executed the data_scientist_agent’s TARGET_SELECT artifact-creation step.

ISSUES:
- No `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_selection.json` has been reported as created.
- No `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_selection_report.md` has been reported as created.
- No `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_diagnostics.json` has been reported as created.
- No `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/plots/target_iri_mean_y_distribution.png` has been reported as created.
- No TARGET_SELECT mm records have been reported for `kind="target_selection"`, `kind="leakage_policy"`, or `kind="pipeline_state"` with `PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT`.
- Reviewer cannot advance state until planner routes TARGET_SELECT execution to coder_agent and coder_agent completes the required artifacts and mm records.

FIX_REQUEST:
- planner_agent must route TARGET_SELECT execution to coder_agent.
- coder_agent must use pinned registry only:
  ```python
  reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
  FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
  DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]
  ```
- coder_agent must create/verify:
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_selection.json`
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_selection_report.md`
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_diagnostics.json`
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/plots/target_iri_mean_y_distribution.png`
  - optional `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/leakage_policy.json`
- `target_selection.json` must specify:
  ```json
  {
    "target_column": "IRI_mean_y",
    "task_type": "regression",
    "target_family": "iri",
    "selected_from": "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/target_candidates.csv"
  }
  ```
- `target_diagnostics.json` must include target dtype, non-null count, missing count, missing percent, min, max, mean, median, standard deviation, and quantiles.
- coder_agent must write mm records:
  - `kind="target_selection"` with text including `TARGET_SELECT selected target IRI_mean_y for regression`
  - `kind="leakage_policy"` with text including `Leakage policy for IRI_mean_y regression target`
  - `kind="pipeline_state"` with text including `PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT`

STATE_NEXT: TARGET_SELECT

----------------------------------------------------------------------
AGENT: planner_agent
----------------------------------------------------------------------
coder_agent: Execute TARGET_SELECT artifact creation/repair now. Stay in `TARGET_SELECT`; do not advance further. Use pinned registry only. Verify DISCOVER_DATASET prerequisites, read approved `target_candidates.csv`, select `IRI_mean_y` as regression target, create required artifacts, write required mm records, and print gate evidence for reviewer.

STATE_TASK: TARGET_SELECT

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

import pandas as pd

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

WORK_DIR = "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace"
STATE_NOW = "TARGET_SELECT"
DISCOVERY_STATE = "DISCOVER_DATASET"

os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)
plots_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "plots"
plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

# Fail-fast governance.
if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir_resolved = Path(WORK_DIR).resolve()
file_path_resolved = Path(FILE_PATH).resolve()

try:
    file_path_resolved.relative_to(work_dir_resolved)
    raise RuntimeError(f"Registry FILE_PATH must not be inside WORK_DIR: {FILE_PATH}")
except ValueError:
    pass

pinned = os.environ.get("PINNED_FILE_PATH")
if pinned and str(Path(pinned).resolve()) != str(file_path_resolved):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "PINNED_FILE_PATH does not match registry latest_path. "
        f"PINNED_FILE_PATH={pinned}; registry latest_path={FILE_PATH}"
    )

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

# Approved DISCOVER_DATASET artifacts.
dataset_profile_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "dataset_profile.json"
dataset_head_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "dataset_head.csv"
target_candidates_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_candidates.csv"

# TARGET_SELECT artifacts.
target_selection_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_selection.json"
target_selection_report_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_selection_report.md"
target_diagnostics_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_diagnostics.json"
leakage_policy_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "leakage_policy.json"
target_plot_path = plots_dir / "target_iri_mean_y_distribution.png"

selected_target = "IRI_mean_y"
task_type = "regression"
target_family = "iri"
required_candidate_schema = [
    "column",
    "family",
    "family_confidence",
    "reasons",
    "leakage_guard",
]

def rag_search(kind, query, k=10):
    return mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=k,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
    )

def has_record(kind, query, k=10):
    return bool(rag_search(kind=kind, query=query, k=k))

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1) Verify DISCOVER_DATASET hard gate before TARGET_SELECT work.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
missing_discovery_gate = []
if not dataset_profile_path.exists():
    missing_discovery_gate.append(str(dataset_profile_path))
if not dataset_head_path.exists():
    missing_discovery_gate.append(str(dataset_head_path))
if not target_candidates_path.exists():
    missing_discovery_gate.append(str(target_candidates_path))

discovery_dataset_artifact_hits = rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET dataset artifacts dataset_profile dataset_head",
    k=10
)
discovery_target_candidate_hits = rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="DISCOVER_DATASET target_candidates.csv",
    k=10
)
discovery_pipeline_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET",
    k=10
)

if not discovery_dataset_artifact_hits:
    missing_discovery_gate.append('mm record kind="dataset_artifact" for DISCOVER_DATASET')
if not discovery_target_candidate_hits:
    missing_discovery_gate.append('mm record kind="target_candidates" for DISCOVER_DATASET')
if not discovery_pipeline_hits:
    missing_discovery_gate.append(
        'mm record kind="pipeline_state" with PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET'
    )

if missing_discovery_gate:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Cannot run TARGET_SELECT because DISCOVER_DATASET hard gate is incomplete. "
        "Missing: " + "; ".join(missing_discovery_gate)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2) Required memory retrievals for traceability/repeat avoidance.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
target_candidates_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="target_candidates.csv",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

dataset_artifact_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="COLUMNS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

model_metrics_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

# Check prior TARGET_SELECT completion.
target_state_hits_before = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
    k=5
)
state_already_completed_before_run = bool(target_state_hits_before)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3) Load approved artifacts and pinned dataset.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
candidate_df = pd.read_csv(target_candidates_path)
if list(candidate_df.columns) != required_candidate_schema:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "target_candidates.csv schema invalid. "
        f"Got {list(candidate_df.columns)}, expected {required_candidate_schema}"
    )
if len(candidate_df) < 1:
    raise RuntimeError("target_candidates.csv is empty.")

candidate_columns = candidate_df["column"].astype(str).tolist()
if selected_target not in candidate_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Selected target {selected_target} is not present in approved target_candidates.csv. "
        f"Candidates: {candidate_columns}"
    )

df = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False)
dataset_columns = [str(c) for c in df.columns.tolist()]

if selected_target not in dataset_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Selected target {selected_target} is not present in dataset columns. "
        f"Columns: {dataset_columns}"
    )

# Target numeric/coercibility validation.
target_raw = df[selected_target]
target_numeric = pd.to_numeric(target_raw, errors="coerce")

raw_non_null_count = int(target_raw.notna().sum())
coerced_non_null_count = int(target_numeric.notna().sum())
coercion_fail_count = int(raw_non_null_count - coerced_non_null_count)

if coerced_non_null_count == 0:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Target {selected_target} has no numeric/coercible non-null values.")

# Conservative numeric/coercible policy: fail if non-null values cannot be coerced.
if coercion_fail_count > 0:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Target {selected_target} contains {coercion_fail_count} non-null values "
        "that cannot be coerced to numeric."
    )

target_missing_count = int(target_numeric.isna().sum())
target_non_null_count = int(target_numeric.notna().sum())
target_missing_pct = float(target_numeric.isna().mean() * 100.0)

quantiles = target_numeric.dropna().quantile([0.01, 0.05, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 0.95, 0.99])

target_diagnostics = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": selected_target,
    "task_type": task_type,
    "target_dtype_original": str(target_raw.dtype),
    "target_dtype_modeling": "numeric",
    "row_count": int(len(df)),
    "non_null_count": target_non_null_count,
    "missing_count": target_missing_count,
    "missing_percent": target_missing_pct,
    "coercion_fail_count": coercion_fail_count,
    "min": float(target_numeric.min(skipna=True)),
    "max": float(target_numeric.max(skipna=True)),
    "mean": float(target_numeric.mean(skipna=True)),
    "median": float(target_numeric.median(skipna=True)),
    "standard_deviation": float(target_numeric.std(skipna=True)),
    "quantiles": {str(k): float(v) for k, v in quantiles.items()},
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4) Define target selection and leakage policy artifacts.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
leakage_policy = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": selected_target,
    "task_type": task_type,
    "banned_features": [
        selected_target
    ],
    "conditionally_allowed_features": [
        "IRI_mean_x"
    ],
    "hard_ban_patterns": [
        "*_y except selected target removed from X",
        "target",
        "label",
        "outcome",
        "post",
        "future_condition",
        "delta_iri",
        "iri_change",
        "improvement",
        "deterioration"
    ],
    "special_handling": {
        "IRI_mean_x": {
            "status": "conditionally_allowed_feature",
            "condition": (
                "May be used only if interpreted as baseline/current/pre-treatment IRI "
                "measured before IRI_mean_y."
            ),
            "fallback_if_temporal_provenance_unclear": [
                "Run full baseline model with IRI_mean_x",
                "Run strict no-prior-IRI model excluding IRI_mean_x"
            ]
        },
        "Future_AADT_mean_x": {
            "status": "review_during_feature_engineering",
            "note": (
                "Contains the word Future but appears to be a traffic forecast variable, "
                "not necessarily pavement condition leakage."
            )
        }
    },
    "target_handling": {
        "missing_target_rows": "drop from supervised modeling; do not impute target",
        "modeling_task": "supervised regression",
        "remove_target_from_features": True
    }
}

competing_candidates = []
for _, row in candidate_df.iterrows():
    col = str(row["column"])
    if col == selected_target:
        continue
    if col == "IRI_mean_x":
        reason = (
            "Not selected because _x suggests baseline/input-side IRI. "
            "It may be conditionally allowed as a prior-condition feature if measured "
            "before IRI_mean_y."
        )
    elif col == "Cracking_Percent_mean_x":
        reason = (
            "Not selected because cracking is an important distress measure but this "
            "_x candidate is better treated as a baseline/input feature for predicting "
            "future ride quality."
        )
    elif col == "Rutting_mean_x":
        reason = (
            "Not selected because rutting is an important distress measure but this "
            "_x candidate is better treated as a baseline/input feature."
        )
    else:
        reason = (
            "Not selected because IRI_mean_y is the recommended outcome-side continuous "
            "IRI target for this pavement condition prediction task."
        )
    competing_candidates.append({
        "column": col,
        "family": str(row.get("family", "")),
        "family_confidence": str(row.get("family_confidence", "")),
        "not_selected_reason": reason,
    })

target_selection = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "created_at_utc": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
    "target_column": selected_target,
    "task_type": task_type,
    "target_family": target_family,
    "selected_from": str(target_candidates_path),
    "selection_reason": (
        "IRI_mean_y is selected as the continuous regression target because IRI is a "
        "standard pavement ride-quality and condition metric, and the _y suffix suggests "
        "the outcome-side or later/post-treatment condition variable in this joined dataset."
    ),
    "selected_candidate_record": candidate_df[
        candidate_df["column"].astype(str) == selected_target
    ].iloc[0].to_dict(),
    "competing_candidates_not_selected": competing_candidates,
    "leakage_policy": leakage_policy,
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5) Save artifacts.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
with open(target_selection_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(target_selection, f, indent=2)

with open(leakage_policy_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(leakage_policy, f, indent=2)

with open(target_diagnostics_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(target_diagnostics, f, indent=2)

# Target distribution plot.
plt.figure(figsize=(9, 6))
plt.hist(target_numeric.dropna(), bins=40, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
plt.xlabel(selected_target)
plt.ylabel("Count")
plt.title(f"Distribution of selected target: {selected_target}")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(target_plot_path, dpi=150)
plt.close()

# Human-readable report.
report_md = f"""# TARGET_SELECT Report

## Selected Target

- Target column: `{selected_target}`
- Task type: `{task_type}`
- Target family: `{target_family}`
- Candidate source: `{target_candidates_path}`

`{selected_target}` is selected as the pavement condition prediction target and should be modeled as a continuous regression target.

## Rationale

IRI / International Roughness Index is a standard pavement ride-quality and condition measure. The `_y` suffix suggests an outcome-side or later-condition variable in this joined dataset, making `{selected_target}` appropriate for future or post-treatment pavement condition prediction.

## Candidate Comparison

Approved target candidates:

{candidate_df.to_markdown(index=False)}

Competing candidates were not selected because `_x` variables are more consistent with baseline/input-side condition features.

## Leakage Controls

- Remove `{selected_target}` from model features.
- Drop rows with missing `{selected_target}` for supervised modeling; do not impute target values.
- Treat `IRI_mean_x` as conditionally allowed only if it is baseline/current/pre-treatment IRI measured before `{selected_target}`.
- If temporal provenance of `IRI_mean_x` is unclear, run:
  1. a full baseline model with `IRI_mean_x`, and
  2. a strict no-prior-IRI model excluding `IRI_mean_x`.
- Exclude non-target `_y` columns from predictors if such columns appear later.

## Target Diagnostics

```json
{json.dumps(target_diagnostics, indent=2)}
```

## Artifacts

- `{target_selection_path}`
- `{target_selection_report_path}`
- `{target_diagnostics_path}`
- `{leakage_policy_path}`
- `{target_plot_path}`
"""

with open(target_selection_report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(report_md)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6) Verify artifacts.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
required_artifacts = [
    target_selection_path,
    target_selection_report_path,
    target_diagnostics_path,
    leakage_policy_path,
    target_plot_path,
]

missing_artifacts = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts if not p.exists()]
if missing_artifacts:
    raise RuntimeError("TARGET_SELECT artifacts missing: " + "; ".join(missing_artifacts))

# Re-load and verify key JSON fields.
with open(target_selection_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    target_selection_loaded = json.load(f)

required_selection_fields = {
    "target_column": selected_target,
    "task_type": task_type,
    "target_family": target_family,
    "selected_from": str(target_candidates_path),
}
for key, expected in required_selection_fields.items():
    got = target_selection_loaded.get(key)
    if got != expected:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"target_selection.json field {key} invalid. Got {got}, expected {expected}"
        )

with open(target_diagnostics_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    diagnostics_loaded = json.load(f)

required_diagnostic_keys = [
    "target_dtype_original",
    "non_null_count",
    "missing_count",
    "missing_percent",
    "min",
    "max",
    "mean",
    "median",
    "standard_deviation",
    "quantiles",
]
missing_diagnostic_keys = [k for k in required_diagnostic_keys if k not in diagnostics_loaded]
if missing_diagnostic_keys:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "target_diagnostics.json missing keys: " + "; ".join(missing_diagnostic_keys)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7) Write/repair required mm records.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
artifacts_saved = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts]

mm.rag_add(
    kind="target_selection",
    text=(
        "TARGET_SELECT selected target IRI_mean_y for regression. "
        f"Target family={target_family}; selected_from={target_candidates_path}; "
        f"artifacts={artifacts_saved}"
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": selected_target,
        "task_type": task_type,
        "target_family": target_family,
        "selected_from": str(target_candidates_path),
        "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
        "target_diagnostics_summary": {
            "non_null_count": target_diagnostics["non_null_count"],
            "missing_count": target_diagnostics["missing_count"],
            "missing_percent": target_diagnostics["missing_percent"],
            "mean": target_diagnostics["mean"],
            "median": target_diagnostics["median"],
            "standard_deviation": target_diagnostics["standard_deviation"],
        },
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="leakage_policy",
    text=(
        "Leakage policy for IRI_mean_y regression target. "
        "Ban target from features; conditionally allow IRI_mean_x only if baseline/pre-outcome; "
        "exclude target-derived or future outcome condition features."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": selected_target,
        "task_type": task_type,
        "banned_features": leakage_policy["banned_features"],
        "conditionally_allowed_features": leakage_policy["conditionally_allowed_features"],
        "artifacts_saved": [str(leakage_policy_path), str(target_selection_path)],
    }
)

# Confirm target_selection and leakage_policy mm records exist before pipeline_state.
target_selection_hits = rag_search(
    kind="target_selection",
    query="TARGET_SELECT selected target IRI_mean_y for regression",
    k=10
)
leakage_policy_hits = rag_search(
    kind="leakage_policy",
    query="Leakage policy for IRI_mean_y regression target",
    k=10
)

if not target_selection_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="target_selection" after write.')
if not leakage_policy_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="leakage_policy" after write.')

mm.rag_add(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    text=(
        "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT. "
        "Selected IRI_mean_y as regression target; target-selection artifacts, "
        "target diagnostics, leakage policy, and target distribution plot verified."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": selected_target,
        "task_type": task_type,
        "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved,
    }
)

pipeline_state_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
    k=10
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8) Final TARGET_SELECT hard gate.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
missing_gate_items = []
for p in required_artifacts:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(str(p))
if not target_selection_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="target_selection"')
if not leakage_policy_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="leakage_policy"')
if not pipeline_state_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append(
        'mm record kind="pipeline_state" with PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT'
    )

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "TARGET_SELECT hard gate failed. Missing: " + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 9) Print reviewer gate evidence.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
gate_report = {
    "status": "TARGET_SELECT_COMPLETED_VERIFIED_PENDING_REVIEWER_APPROVAL",
    "DATASET_ID": DATASET_ID,
    "FILE_PATH": FILE_PATH,
    "file_exists": os.path.exists(FILE_PATH),
    "file_path_outside_WORK_DIR": True,
    "PINNED_FILE_PATH_matches_registry": (
        True if not pinned else str(Path(pinned).resolve()) == str(file_path_resolved)
    ),
    "state_already_completed_before_run": state_already_completed_before_run,
    "selected_target": selected_target,
    "task_type": task_type,
    "target_family": target_family,
    "dataset_shape": [int(df.shape[0]), int(df.shape[1])],
    "dataset_columns_as_List_str": dataset_columns,

do not impute target values.\\n- Treat `IRI_mean_x` as conditionally allowed only if it is baseline/current/pre-treatment IRI measured before `{selected_target}`.\\n- If temporal provenance of `IRI_mean_x` is unclear, run:\\n  1. a full baseline model with `IRI_mean_x`, and\\n  2. a strict no-prior-IRI model excluding `IRI_mean_x`.\\n- Exclude non-target `_y` columns from predictors if such columns appear later.\\n\\n## Target Diagnostics\\n\\n```json\\n{json.dumps(target_diagnostics, indent=2)}\\n```\\n\\n## Artifacts\\n\\n- `{target_selection_path}`\\n- `{target_selection_report_path}`\\n- `{target_diagnostics_path}`\\n- `{target_diagnostics_csv_path}`\\n- `{leakage_policy_path}`\\n- `{target_plot_path}`\\n\\"\\"\\"\\n\\nwith open(target_selection_report_path, \\"w\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as f:\\n    f.write(report_md)\\n\\n# Save the complete executed code under WORK_DIR/final_code.py.\\ntry:\\n    with open(__file__, \\"r\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as src:\\n        executed_source = src.read()\\n    with open(final_code_path, \\"w\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as dst:\\n        dst.write(executed_source)\\nexcept Exception as exc:\\n    raise RuntimeError(f\\"Failed to save complete executed code to {final_code_path}: {exc}\\")\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6) Verify artifacts.\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\nrequired_artifacts = [\\n    target_selection_path,\\n    target_selection_report_path,\\n    target_diagnostics_path,\\n    target_diagnostics_csv_path,\\n    leakage_policy_path,\\n    target_plot_path,\\n    final_code_path,\\n]\\n\\nmissing_artifacts = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts if not p.exists()]\\nif missing_artifacts:\\n    raise RuntimeError(\\"TARGET_SELECT artifacts missing: \\" + \\"; 1. Verify approved upstream artifacts.
2. Load the pinned dataset through registry only.
3. Enforce `IRI_mean_y` as the regression target.
4. Exclude `IRI_mean_y` from all model features.
5. Audit columns for leakage and modeling role.
6. Define feature groups.
7. Produce EDA diagnostics and plots.
8. Produce a preprocessing / feature-engineering specification for later model training.
9. Avoid fitting final imputers, scalers, encoders, or models in this state. Those must be fit inside training/CV pipelines later.

# 4. Required leakage controls

The EDA / feature-engineering design must enforce the approved leakage policy.

## Hard exclusions from model features

Always exclude:

```text
IRI_mean_y
```

Also exclude any future columns, if present later, matching these concepts:

```text
non-target *_y variables
target-derived variables
delta_iri
iri_change
improvement
deterioration
post-outcome condition variables
label
target
outcome
```

Current approved dataset has only one `_y` column:

```text
IRI_mean_y
```

It is the target and must never be included in `X`.

## Conditional feature

```text
IRI_mean_x
```

Status:

```text
conditionally_allowed_baseline_feature
```

Allowed only if interpreted as baseline/current/pre-outcome IRI measured before `IRI_mean_y`.

Because temporal provenance may be uncertain, downstream modeling should use two feature sets:

### A. Baseline-condition feature set

Includes `IRI_mean_x`.

Purpose: best predictive performance if baseline IRI is legitimately available before prediction.

### B. Strict no-prior-IRI feature set

Excludes `IRI_mean_x`.

Purpose: leakage sensitivity / deployment-safe comparison if baseline IRI timing is uncertain.

## Availability-sensitive feature

```text
Future_AADT_mean_x
```

Do not automatically ban it solely because it contains `"Future"`.

Recommended status:

```text
availability_sensitive_feature
```

It may be a traffic forecast available at planning time, not an outcome condition variable. The artifact should flag it for later modeling sensitivity analysis.

---

# 5. Feature groups

`coder_agent` should create a machine-readable feature-group artifact using the following recommended groups.

## Traffic / loading

```text
AADT_mean_x
AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x
AADT_Combination_mean_x
Future_AADT_mean_x
```

Potential deterministic engineered features:

```text
single_unit_aadt_share = AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
combination_aadt_share = AADT_Combination_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
future_aadt_ratio = Future_AADT_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
```

Use safe division with zero / missing handling. These are feature-only transformations and do not use the target.

## Pavement structure

```text
Thickness_Rigid_mean_x
Thickness_Flexible_mean_x
Base_Thickness_mean_x
Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x
Surface_Type_mode
Base_Type_mode_x
```

Potential deterministic engineered features:

```text
total_structural_thickness = Thickness_Rigid_mean_x + Thickness_Flexible_mean_x + Base_Thickness_mean_x
has_rigid_thickness = indicator(Thickness_Rigid_mean_x > 0)
has_flexible_thickness = indicator(Thickness_Flexible_mean_x > 0)
```

## Baseline condition

```text
IRI_mean_x
Rutting_mean_x
Cracking_Percent_mean_x
Faulting_mean_x
```

Leakage note:

- `IRI_mean_x` must be tagged conditional.
- Rutting, cracking, and faulting are acceptable baseline condition features if measured before the target.

## Treatment

```text
Treatment_type
Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x
```

`Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x` may also appear under pavement structure. Multiple group memberships are acceptable if documented.

## Climate / environment

```text
RHU_AV_x
FRZ_IDX_x
TEMP_AVG_x
PRECIPITATION_x
```

Potential deterministic engineered features:

```text
freeze_precip_interaction = FRZ_IDX_x * PRECIPITATION_x
temperature_freeze_interaction = TEMP_AVG_x * FRZ_IDX_x
```

Use only feature columns.

## Age / system / context

```text
Age_x
F_System_mode
Urban_Type
```

---

# 6. Preprocessing plan for later modeling

This state should define preprocessing but not fit final preprocessors to all data.

Recommended downstream preprocessing:

## Numeric features

- Convert numeric columns with `pd.to_numeric(errors="coerce")`.
- Impute missing numeric values inside training/CV pipeline using median imputation.
- Optionally apply scaling for linear/kernel/distance-sensitive models.
- Tree-based models may not require scaling.

## Categorical features

Categorical columns expected:

```text
F_System_mode
Urban_Type
Surface_Type_mode
Base_Type_mode_x
Treatment_type
```

Recommended handling:

- Cast to string/category.
- Impute missing values as `"Unknown"` or use most-frequent imputation inside training pipeline.
- One-hot encode with `handle_unknown="ignore"` for sklearn-compatible models.
- For models that natively support categorical variables, preserve categorical metadata in a separate model-specific plan, but still produce a one-hot-compatible default plan.

## Target

- `IRI_mean_y` remains numeric regression target.
- Drop rows with missing target for supervised modeling.
- Do not impute the target.
- Target should not be scaled globally in this EDA state. If target transformations are tested later, they must occur inside model evaluation logic and be reported clearly.

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json

import pandas as pd

import matplotlib

STATE_NOW = "EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN"
TARGET_COLUMN = "IRI_mean_y"
TASK_TYPE = "regression"

plots_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "plots"
plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

# Fail-fast governance.
if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir_resolved = Path(WORK_DIR).resolve()
file_path_resolved = Path(FILE_PATH).resolve()

try:
    file_path_resolved.relative_to(work_dir_resolved)
    raise RuntimeError(f"Registry FILE_PATH must not be inside WORK_DIR: {FILE_PATH}")
except ValueError:
    pass

pinned = os.environ.get("PINNED_FILE_PATH")
if pinned and str(Path(pinned).resolve()) != str(file_path_resolved):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "PINNED_FILE_PATH does not match registry latest_path. "
        f"PINNED_FILE_PATH={pinned}; registry latest_path={FILE_PATH}"
    )

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

# Approved upstream artifacts.
dataset_profile_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "dataset_profile.json"
target_selection_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_selection.json"
leakage_policy_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "leakage_policy.json"

preprocessing_plan_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "preprocessing_plan.json"

# Required plots.
