def rag_search(kind, query, k=10):
    return mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=k,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
    )

def safe_float(x):
    if x is None:
        return None
    try:
        if pd.isna(x):
            return None
        return float(x)
    except Exception:
        return None

def safe_int(x):
    if x is None:
        return None
    try:
        if pd.isna(x):
            return None
        return int(x)
    except Exception:
        return None

def existing(cols):
    return [c for c in cols if c in dataset_columns]

def save_placeholder_plot(path, title, message):
    plt.figure(figsize=(9, 5))
    plt.text(0.5, 0.5, message, ha="center", va="center", wrap=True)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close()

def plot_scatter_if_possible(x_col, y_col, path, title):
    if x_col in df.columns and y_col in df.columns:
        x = pd.to_numeric(df[x_col], errors="coerce")
        y = pd.to_numeric(df[y_col], errors="coerce")
        mask = x.notna() & y.notna()
        if int(mask.sum()) > 0:
            plt.figure(figsize=(7, 6))
            plt.scatter(x[mask], y[mask], alpha=0.35, s=14)
            plt.xlabel(x_col)
            plt.ylabel(y_col)
            plt.title(title)
            plt.tight_layout()
            plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
            plt.close()
            return
    save_placeholder_plot(path, title, f"{x_col} or {y_col} unavailable/non-numeric.")

def plot_box_by_category(cat_col, y_col, path, title, max_categories=15):
    if cat_col in df.columns and y_col in df.columns:
        y = pd.to_numeric(df[y_col], errors="coerce")
        temp = pd.DataFrame({
            cat_col: df[cat_col].astype("object").where(df[cat_col].notna(), "Unknown").astype(str),
            y_col: y
        }).dropna(subset=[y_col])
        if len(temp) > 0:
            top_cats = temp[cat_col].value_counts().head(max_categories).index.tolist()
            temp = temp[temp[cat_col].isin(top_cats)]
            groups = [temp.loc[temp[cat_col] == c, y_col].values for c in top_cats]
            if groups:
                plt.figure(figsize=(max(9, len(top_cats) * 0.7), 6))
                plt.boxplot(groups, labels=top_cats, showfliers=False)
                plt.xticks(rotation=45, ha="right")
                plt.ylabel(y_col)
                plt.title(title)
                plt.tight_layout()
                plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
                plt.close()
                return
    save_placeholder_plot(path, title, f"{cat_col} unavailable or no usable target values.")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1) Required memory retrievals and upstream gate checks.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
target_candidates_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="target_candidates.csv",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

dataset_artifact_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="COLUMNS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

model_metrics_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

discover_pipeline_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET",
    k=10
)

target_select_pipeline_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
    k=10
)

target_selection_hits_prior = rag_search(
    kind="target_selection",
    query="TARGET_SELECT selected target IRI_mean_y for regression",
    k=10
)

leakage_policy_hits_prior = rag_search(
    kind="leakage_policy",
    query="Leakage policy for IRI_mean_y regression target",
    k=10
)

eda_state_hits_before = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN",
    k=5
)
state_already_completed_before_run = bool(eda_state_hits_before)

missing_upstream = []
for p in approved_artifact_paths:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_upstream.append(str(p))
if not discover_pipeline_hits:
    missing_upstream.append('mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET')
if not target_select_pipeline_hits:
    missing_upstream.append('mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT')
if not target_selection_hits_prior:
    missing_upstream.append('mm record kind="target_selection"')
if not leakage_policy_hits_prior:
    missing_upstream.append('mm record kind="leakage_policy"')

if missing_upstream:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Cannot run EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN because upstream gates are incomplete. "
        "Missing: " + "; ".join(missing_upstream)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2) Load approved artifacts and pinned dataset.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
with open(target_selection_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    target_selection = json.load(f)

with open(leakage_policy_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    leakage_policy = json.load(f)

with open(target_diagnostics_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    target_diagnostics = json.load(f)

with open(dataset_profile_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    dataset_profile = json.load(f)

target_candidates_df = pd.read_csv(target_candidates_path)
expected_candidate_schema = ["column", "family", "family_confidence", "reasons", "leakage_guard"]
if list(target_candidates_df.columns) != expected_candidate_schema:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "target_candidates.csv schema invalid. "
        f"Got {list(target_candidates_df.columns)}, expected {expected_candidate_schema}"
    )

if target_selection.get("target_column") != TARGET_COLUMN:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"target_selection.json target_column invalid: {target_selection.get('target_column')}"
    )
if target_selection.get("task_type") != TASK_TYPE:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"target_selection.json task_type invalid: {target_selection.get('task_type')}"
    )

if TARGET_COLUMN not in leakage_policy.get("banned_features", []):
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"leakage_policy.json must include {TARGET_COLUMN} in banned_features."
    )

df = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False)
dataset_columns = [str(c) for c in df.columns.tolist()]
df.columns = dataset_columns

if TARGET_COLUMN not in dataset_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Dataset does not contain required target column {TARGET_COLUMN}.")

target_numeric = pd.to_numeric(df[TARGET_COLUMN], errors="coerce")
if int(target_numeric.notna().sum()) == 0:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Target {TARGET_COLUMN} has no numeric/coercible non-null values.")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3) Define leakage controls, feature groups, feature sets.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
traffic_loading = existing([
    "AADT_mean_x",
    "AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x",
    "AADT_Combination_mean_x",
    "Future_AADT_mean_x",
])

pavement_structure = existing([
    "Thickness_Rigid_mean_x",
    "Thickness_Flexible_mean_x",
    "Base_Thickness_mean_x",
    "Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x",
    "Surface_Type_mode",
    "Base_Type_mode_x",
])

baseline_condition = existing([
    "IRI_mean_x",
    "Rutting_mean_x",
    "Cracking_Percent_mean_x",
    "Faulting_mean_x",
])

treatment = existing([
    "Treatment_type",
    "Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x",
])

climate_environment = existing([
    "RHU_AV_x",
    "FRZ_IDX_x",
    "TEMP_AVG_x",
    "PRECIPITATION_x",
])

age_system_context = existing([
    "Age_x",
    "F_System_mode",
    "Urban_Type",
])

availability_sensitive = existing([
    "Future_AADT_mean_x",
])

conditionally_allowed_features = existing([
    "IRI_mean_x",
])

non_target_y_columns_found = [
    c for c in dataset_columns
    if c.endswith("_y") and c != TARGET_COLUMN
]

target_derived_patterns = [
    "target",
    "label",
    "outcome",
    "post",
    "future_condition",
    "delta_iri",
    "iri_change",
    "improvement",
    "deterioration",
]

pattern_excluded_columns = []
for c in dataset_columns:
    cl = c.lower()
    if c == TARGET_COLUMN:
        continue
    if c in non_target_y_columns_found:
        continue
    if any(pattern in cl for pattern in target_derived_patterns):
        # Do not auto-ban Future_AADT_mean_x solely because it contains "Future";
        # the pattern list intentionally uses "future_condition", not generic "future".
        pattern_excluded_columns.append(c)

leakage_banned = sorted(set([TARGET_COLUMN] + non_target_y_columns_found + pattern_excluded_columns))

all_feature_candidates = [c for c in dataset_columns if c not in leakage_banned]
baseline_condition_allowed = [c for c in all_feature_candidates if c != TARGET_COLUMN]
strict_no_prior_iri = [c for c in baseline_condition_allowed if c != "IRI_mean_x"]

if TARGET_COLUMN in baseline_condition_allowed or TARGET_COLUMN in strict_no_prior_iri:
    raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} was incorrectly included in feature sets.")

feature_sets = {
    "baseline_condition_allowed": baseline_condition_allowed,
    "strict_no_prior_iri": strict_no_prior_iri,
}

feature_groups = {
    "traffic_loading": traffic_loading,
    "pavement_structure": pavement_structure,
    "baseline_condition": baseline_condition,
    "treatment": treatment,
    "climate_environment": climate_environment,
    "age_system_context": age_system_context,
    "availability_sensitive": availability_sensitive,
    "leakage_banned": leakage_banned,
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4) Build feature audit.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
dtype_map = {str(k): str(v) for k, v in df.dtypes.items()}
missing_counts = df.isna().sum()
missing_pcts = df.isna().mean() * 100.0

group_membership = {}
for group_name, cols in feature_groups.items():
    for col in cols:
        group_membership.setdefault(col, []).append(group_name)

audit_rows = []
for col in dataset_columns:
    groups = group_membership.get(col, [])
    if col == TARGET_COLUMN:
        role = "target"
        leakage_status = "target_excluded_from_features"
        action = "use_as_y_only"
        notes = "Approved continuous regression target; never include in X."
    elif col in non_target_y_columns_found:
        role = "excluded_leakage"
        leakage_status = "non_target_y_column"
        action = "exclude_from_all_feature_sets"
        notes = "Non-target _y variable treated as potential outcome-side leakage."
    elif col in pattern_excluded_columns:
        role = "excluded_leakage"
        leakage_status = "target_or_outcome_pattern_match"
        action = "exclude_from_all_feature_sets"
        notes = "Column name matched target-derived/outcome leakage pattern."
    elif col == "IRI_mean_x":
        role = "conditionally_allowed_feature"
        leakage_status = "conditionally_allowed_baseline_feature"
        action = "include in baseline_condition_allowed; exclude in strict_no_prior_iri"
        notes = "Allowed only if baseline/current/pre-outcome IRI measured before IRI_mean_y."
    elif col == "Future_AADT_mean_x":
        role = "availability_sensitive_feature"
        leakage_status = "availability_sensitive_not_auto_banned"
        action = "include with later availability sensitivity review"
        notes = "Traffic forecast may be available at planning time; do not auto-ban solely due to Future."
    else:
        role = "candidate_feature"
        leakage_status = "allowed_if_available_pre_outcome"
        action = "include in both feature sets"
        notes = "Candidate predictor subject to downstream preprocessing and train/CV-only fitting."
    
    audit_rows.append({
        "column": col,
        "dtype": dtype_map.get(col, ""),
        "missing_count": int(missing_counts[col]),
        "missing_percent": float(missing_pcts[col]),
        "role": role,
        "feature_group": ";".join(groups) if groups else "",
        "leakage_status": leakage_status,
        "action": action,
        "notes": notes,
    })

feature_audit_df = pd.DataFrame(audit_rows)
feature_audit_df.to_csv(feature_audit_path, index=False)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5) Preprocessing and feature-engineering plans.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
categorical_expected = existing([
    "F_System_mode",
    "Urban_Type",
    "Surface_Type_mode",
    "Base_Type_mode_x",
    "Treatment_type",
])

numeric_columns = []
for c in baseline_condition_allowed:
    if c not in categorical_expected:
        # Include numeric/coercible columns in default numeric plan.
        coerced = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce")
        if coerced.notna().sum() > 0:
            numeric_columns.append(c)

categorical_columns = [c for c in categorical_expected if c in baseline_condition_allowed]

engineered_feature_rules = {
    "traffic_loading": {
        "single_unit_aadt_share": {
            "formula": "AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x / AADT_mean_x",
            "safe_division": True,
            "required_columns": existing(["AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x", "AADT_mean_x"]),
        },
        "combination_aadt_share": {
            "formula": "AADT_Combination_mean_x / AADT_mean_x",
            "safe_division": True,
            "required_columns": existing(["AADT_Combination_mean_x", "AADT_mean_x"]),
        },
        "future_aadt_ratio": {
            "formula": "Future_AADT_mean_x / AADT_mean_x",
            "safe_division": True,
            "required_columns": existing(["Future_AADT_mean_x", "AADT_mean_x"]),
            "availability_status": "availability_sensitive_feature",
        },
    },
    "pavement_structure": {
        "total_structural_thickness": {
            "formula": "Thickness_Rigid_mean_x + Thickness_Flexible_mean_x + Base_Thickness_mean_x",
            "required_columns": existing([
                "Thickness_Rigid_mean_x",
                "Thickness_Flexible_mean_x",
                "Base_Thickness_mean_x",
            ]),
        },
        "has_rigid_thickness": {
            "formula": "indicator(Thickness_Rigid_mean_x > 0)",
            "required_columns": existing(["Thickness_Rigid_mean_x"]),
        },
        "has_flexible_thickness": {
            "formula": "indicator(Thickness_Flexible_mean_x > 0)",
            "required_columns": existing(["Thickness_Flexible_mean_x"]),
        },
    },
    "climate_environment": {
        "freeze_precip_interaction": {
            "formula": "FRZ_IDX_x * PRECIPITATION_x",
            "required_columns": existing(["FRZ_IDX_x", "PRECIPITATION_x"]),
        },
        "temperature_freeze_interaction": {
            "formula": "TEMP_AVG_x * FRZ_IDX_x",
            "required_columns": existing(["TEMP_AVG_x", "FRZ_IDX_x"]),
        },
    },
    "leakage_rule": "Engineered features must use feature columns only and never use IRI_mean_y.",
}

preprocessing_plan = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "numeric_columns": numeric_columns,
    "categorical_columns": categorical_columns,
    "numeric_imputation_strategy": "median imputation inside training/CV pipeline only",
    "categorical_imputation_strategy": "Unknown or most-frequent imputation inside training/CV pipeline only",
    "categorical_encoding_strategy": "one-hot encoding with handle_unknown='ignore' for sklearn-compatible default plan",
    "scaling_strategy": "optional scaling for linear/kernel/distance-sensitive models; tree models may not require scaling",
    "target_handling": "Drop rows with missing target; do not impute target.",
    "where_to_fit_preprocessors": (
        "Fit imputers/scalers/encoders only inside train/CV folds during modeling, "
        "not on the full dataset in EDA."
    ),
    "modeling_feature_sets": feature_sets,
    "engineered_feature_rules": engineered_feature_rules,
}

leakage_screening = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "banned_features": leakage_banned,
    "conditionally_allowed_features": conditionally_allowed_features,
    "availability_sensitive_features": availability_sensitive,
    "non_target_y_columns_found": non_target_y_columns_found,
    "target_derived_patterns_checked": target_derived_patterns,
    "pattern_excluded_columns": pattern_excluded_columns,
    "final_excluded_columns": leakage_banned,
    "feature_set_baseline_allowed": baseline_condition_allowed,
    "feature_set_strict_no_prior_iri": strict_no_prior_iri,
    "leakage_notes": {
        "IRI_mean_x": (
            "High association with IRI_mean_y does not alone prove leakage, but it requires "
            "temporal provenance review and two-feature-set modeling."
        ),
        "Future_AADT_mean_x": (
            "Availability-sensitive traffic forecast; review whether it is available at prediction time."
        ),
    },
}

eda_feature_engineering_plan = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "created_at_utc": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "feature_sets": feature_sets,
    "preprocessing_plan": preprocessing_plan,
    "feature_engineering_rules": engineered_feature_rules,
    "leakage_controls": leakage_screening,
    "no_model_training_in_this_state": True,
    "fit_final_preprocessors_only_inside_train_cv_folds": True,
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6) EDA summaries.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
numeric_df = pd.DataFrame()
for c in dataset_columns:
    coerced = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce")
    if coerced.notna().sum() > 0:
        numeric_df[c] = coerced

numeric_summary = {}
for c in numeric_df.columns:
    s = numeric_df[c]
    numeric_summary[c] = {
        "non_null_count": int(s.notna().sum()),
        "missing_count": int(s.isna().sum()),
        "mean": safe_float(s.mean(skipna=True)),
        "median": safe_float(s.median(skipna=True)),
        "std": safe_float(s.std(skipna=True)),
        "min": safe_float(s.min(skipna=True)),
        "max": safe_float(s.max(skipna=True)),
        "q25": safe_float(s.quantile(0.25)),
        "q75": safe_float(s.quantile(0.75)),
    }

categorical_summary = {}
for c in categorical_columns:
    vc = df[c].astype("object").where(df[c].notna(), "Unknown").astype(str).value_counts(dropna=False).head(20)
    categorical_summary[c] = {
        "unique_count": int(df[c].nunique(dropna=True)),
        "top_values": {str(k): int(v) for k, v in vc.items()},
    }

missingness_summary = [
    {
        "column": str(c),
        "missing_count": int(missing_counts[c]),
        "missing_percent": float(missing_pcts[c]),
    }
    for c in missing_counts.sort_values(ascending=False).index
]

target_summary = {
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "dtype_original": str(df[TARGET_COLUMN].dtype),
    "non_null_count": int(target_numeric.notna().sum()),
    "missing_count": int(target_numeric.isna().sum()),
    "missing_percent": float(target_numeric.isna().mean() * 100.0),
    "min": safe_float(target_numeric.min(skipna=True)),
    "max": safe_float(target_numeric.max(skipna=True)),
    "mean": safe_float(target_numeric.mean(skipna=True)),
    "median": safe_float(target_numeric.median(skipna=True)),
    "standard_deviation": safe_float(target_numeric.std(skipna=True)),
    "quantiles": {
        "0.01": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.01)),
        "0.05": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.05)),
        "0.25": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.25)),
        "0.50": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.50)),
        "0.75": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.75)),
        "0.95": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.95)),
        "0.99": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.99)),
    },
}

correlation_with_target = {}
if TARGET_COLUMN in numeric_df.columns:
    for c in numeric_df.columns:
        if c == TARGET_COLUMN:
            continue
        valid = numeric_df[[c, TARGET_COLUMN]].dropna()
        if len(valid) >= 3:
            corr = valid[c].corr(valid[TARGET_COLUMN])
            correlation_with_target[c] = safe_float(corr)

correlation_with_target_sorted = dict(
    sorted(
        correlation_with_target.items(),
        key=lambda kv: abs(kv[1]) if kv[1] is not None else -1,
        reverse=True
    )
)

outlier_summary = {}
for c in numeric_df.columns:
    s = numeric_df[c].dropna()
    if len(s) > 0:
        q1 = s.quantile(0.25)
        q3 = s.quantile(0.75)
        iqr = q3 - q1
        if pd.isna(iqr) or iqr == 0:
            lower = None
            upper = None
            outlier_count = 0
        else:
            lower = q1 - 1.5 * iqr
            upper = q3 + 1.5 * iqr
            outlier_count = int(((s < lower) | (s > upper)).sum())
        outlier_summary[c] = {
            "iqr_lower_fence": safe_float(lower),
            "iqr_upper_fence": safe_float(upper),
            "iqr_outlier_count": outlier_count,
            "iqr_outlier_percent_non_null": safe_float(outlier_count / len(s) * 100.0),
        }

eda_summary = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "dataset_shape": {
        "rows": int(df.shape[0]),
        "columns": int(df.shape[1]),
    },
    "dataset_columns": dataset_columns,
    "target_summary": target_summary,
    "numeric_summary": numeric_summary,
    "categorical_summary": categorical_summary,
    "missingness_summary": missingness_summary,
    "correlation_with_target": correlation_with_target_sorted,
    "outlier_summary": outlier_summary,
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7) Save JSON/CSV/Markdown artifacts.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
with open(feature_groups_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump({
        "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
        "file_path": FILE_PATH,
        "state": STATE_NOW,
        "feature_groups": feature_groups,
    }, f, indent=2)

with open(preprocessing_plan_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(preprocessing_plan, f, indent=2)

with open(leakage_screening_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(leakage_screening, f, indent=2)

with open(eda_plan_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(eda_feature_engineering_plan, f, indent=2)

with open(eda_summary_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(eda_summary, f, indent=2)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8) Create required EDA plots.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# Missingness by column.
missing_plot_data = missing_pcts.sort_values(ascending=True)
plt.figure(figsize=(10, max(6, len(missing_plot_data) * 0.28)))
plt.barh([str(x) for x in missing_plot_data.index], missing_plot_data.values)
plt.xlabel("Missing values (%)")
plt.ylabel("Column")
plt.title("EDA Missingness by Column")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_missingness_by_column"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

# Numeric missingness bar.
numeric_missing = numeric_df.isna().mean().sort_values(ascending=True) * 100.0
if len(numeric_missing) > 0:
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, max(6, len(numeric_missing) * 0.28)))
    plt.barh([str(x) for x in numeric_missing.index], numeric_missing.values)
    plt.xlabel("Missing numeric values after coercion (%)")
    plt.ylabel("Numeric/coercible column")
    plt.title("EDA Numeric Missingness")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_numeric_missingness_matrix_or_bar"], dpi=150)
    plt.close()
else:
    save_placeholder_plot(
        plot_paths["eda_numeric_missingness_matrix_or_bar"],
        "EDA Numeric Missingness",
        "No numeric/coercible columns found."
    )

# Target distribution.
plt.figure(figsize=(9, 6))
plt.hist(target_numeric.dropna(), bins=40, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
plt.xlabel(TARGET_COLUMN)
plt.ylabel("Count")
plt.title(f"EDA Target Distribution: {TARGET_COLUMN}")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_target_distribution_iri_mean_y"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

# Target boxplot.
plt.figure(figsize=(8, 4))
plt.boxplot(target_numeric.dropna(), vert=False, showfliers=True)
plt.xlabel(TARGET_COLUMN)
plt.title(f"EDA Target Boxplot: {TARGET_COLUMN}")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_target_boxplot_iri_mean_y"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

# Numeric feature distributions.
dist_cols = [c for c in numeric_columns if c != TARGET_COLUMN][:20]
if dist_cols:
    n = len(dist_cols)
    ncols = 4
    nrows = math.ceil(n / ncols)
    plt.figure(figsize=(16, max(4, nrows * 3)))
    for idx, c in enumerate(dist_cols, start=1):
        plt.subplot(nrows, ncols, idx)
        vals = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors="coerce").dropna()
        if len(vals) > 0:
            plt.hist(vals, bins=25, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
        plt.title(c, fontsize=9)
        plt.xticks(fontsize=7)
        plt.yticks(fontsize=7)
    plt.suptitle("EDA Numeric Feature Distributions", y=1.02)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_numeric_feature_distributions"], dpi=150, bbox_inches="tight")
    plt.close()
else:
    save_placeholder_plot(
        plot_paths["eda_numeric_feature_distributions"],
        "EDA Numeric Feature Distributions",
        "No numeric feature columns available."
    )

# Correlation heatmap using numeric columns including target for EDA association only.
corr_cols = [c for c in numeric_df.columns if c in baseline_condition_allowed or c == TARGET_COLUMN]
corr_cols = corr_cols[:30]
if len(corr_cols) >= 2:
    corr = numeric_df[corr_cols].corr()
    plt.figure(figsize=(max(8, len(corr_cols) * 0.45), max(7, len(corr_cols) * 0.45)))
    im = plt.imshow(corr.values, aspect="auto", cmap="coolwarm", vmin=-1, vmax=1)
    plt.colorbar(im, fraction=0.046, pad=0.04)
    plt.xticks(range(len(corr_cols)), corr_cols, rotation=90, fontsize=7)
    plt.yticks(range(len(corr_cols)), corr_cols, fontsize=7)
    plt.title("EDA Numeric Correlation Heatmap")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_numeric_correlation_heatmap"], dpi=150)
    plt.close()
else:
    save_placeholder_plot(
        plot_paths["eda_numeric_correlation_heatmap"],
        "EDA Numeric Correlation Heatmap",
        "Fewer than two numeric columns available."
    )

# Target-numeric correlations.
if correlation_with_target_sorted:
    corr_series = pd.Series(correlation_with_target_sorted).dropna()
    corr_series = corr_series.reindex(corr_series.abs().sort_values(ascending=True).index).tail(25)
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, max(6, len(corr_series) * 0.3)))
    plt.barh(corr_series.index.astype(str), corr_series.values)
    plt.xlabel(f"Pearson correlation with {TARGET_COLUMN}")
    plt.ylabel("Numeric feature")
    plt.title("EDA Target-Numeric Correlations")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(plot_paths["eda_target_numeric_correlations"], dpi=150)
    plt.close()
else:
    save_placeholder_plot(
        plot_paths["eda_target_numeric_correlations"],
        "EDA Target-Numeric Correlations",
        "No usable numeric target correlations."
    )

# Baseline IRI diagnostic and condition distress plots.
plot_scatter_if_possible(
    "IRI_mean_x",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_iri_mean_x_vs_iri_mean_y"],
    "EDA Baseline IRI vs Target IRI"
)
plot_scatter_if_possible(
    "Rutting_mean_x",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_vs_rutting"],
    "EDA Target IRI vs Rutting"
)
plot_scatter_if_possible(
    "Cracking_Percent_mean_x",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_vs_cracking"],
    "EDA Target IRI vs Cracking Percent"
)
plot_scatter_if_possible(
    "Faulting_mean_x",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_vs_faulting"],
    "EDA Target IRI vs Faulting"
)

# Categorical/context plots.
plot_box_by_category(
    "Treatment_type",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_by_treatment_type"],
    "EDA Target IRI by Treatment Type"
)
plot_box_by_category(
    "Surface_Type_mode",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_by_surface_type"],
    "EDA Target IRI by Surface Type"
)
plot_box_by_category(
    "Urban_Type",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_by_urban_type"],
    "EDA Target IRI by Urban Type"
)
plot_box_by_category(
    "F_System_mode",
    TARGET_COLUMN,
    plot_paths["eda_target_by_functional_system"],
    "EDA Target IRI by Functional System"
)

plot_paths_saved = [str(p) for p in plot_paths.values() if p.exists()]

# Human-readable report.
top_corr_lines = []
for col, val in list(correlation_with_target_sorted.items())[:10]:
    top_corr_lines.append(f"- `{col}`: {val:.4f}" if val is not None else f"- `{col}`: null")
top_corr_text = "\n".join(top_corr_lines) if top_corr_lines else "- No usable numeric correlations."

eda_report = f"""# EDA / Feature Engineering Design Report

## State

`{STATE_NOW}`

No model training, final preprocessing fitting, or target transformation fitting was performed in this state.

## Target and Task

- Target column: `{TARGET_COLUMN}`
- Task type: `{TASK_TYPE}`
- Target use: numeric continuous pavement IRI / ride-quality prediction target
- Feature rule: `{TARGET_COLUMN}` is excluded from all model feature sets.

## Dataset

- Dataset ID: `{DATASET_ID}`
- File path: `{FILE_PATH}`
- Shape: `{df.shape[0]}` rows x `{df.shape[1]}` columns

## Leakage Controls

Hard-excluded columns:

```json
{json.dumps(leakage_banned, indent=2)}
```

Conditionally allowed features:

```json
{json.dumps(conditionally_allowed_features, indent=2)}
```

Availability-sensitive features:

```json
{json.dumps(availability_sensitive, indent=2)}
```

`IRI_mean_x` is conditionally allowed only as a baseline/current/pre-outcome IRI feature. High correlation between `IRI_mean_x` and `IRI_mean_y` does not by itself prove leakage, but it increases the need for two-feature-set modeling.

`Future_AADT_mean_x` is not automatically banned solely because it contains `Future`; it is flagged for later availability-at-prediction-time review.

## Feature Sets

### baseline_condition_allowed

Includes valid pre-outcome features, including conditionally allowed `IRI_mean_x`.

```json
{json.dumps(baseline_condition_allowed, indent=2)}
```

### strict_no_prior_iri

Excludes both `{TARGET_COLUMN}` and `IRI_mean_x`.

```json
{json.dumps(strict_no_prior_iri, indent=2)}
```

## Feature Groups

```json
{json.dumps(feature_groups, indent=2)}
```

## Preprocessing Plan

- Numeric imputation: median, fit inside training/CV folds only.
- Categorical imputation: `"Unknown"` or most frequent, fit inside training/CV folds only.
- Categorical encoding: one-hot encoding with `handle_unknown='ignore'`.
- Scaling: optional for linear/kernel/distance-sensitive models.
- Target: drop missing target rows; do not impute target.

## Top Numeric Correlations with Target

These correlations are for EDA association analysis only. `{TARGET_COLUMN}` must not be used as a feature.

{top_corr_text}

## Artifacts

- EDA plan: `{eda_plan_path}`
- Feature groups: `{feature_groups_path}`
- Feature audit: `{feature_audit_path}`
- Leakage screening: `{leakage_screening_path}`
- Preprocessing plan: `{preprocessing_plan_path}`
- EDA summary: `{eda_summary_path}`
- Plot files: `{plots_dir}`

## Recommended Next Modeling Approach

Train and evaluate at least two model feature sets later:

1. `baseline_condition_allowed`: includes `IRI_mean_x`.
2. `strict_no_prior_iri`: excludes `IRI_mean_x`.

All preprocessing and feature engineering must be implemented inside modeling pipelines and fit only on train/CV folds.
"""

with open(eda_report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(eda_report)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 9) Verify required artifacts and plots.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
required_artifacts = [
    eda_plan_path,
    feature_groups_path,
    feature_audit_path,
    leakage_screening_path,
    preprocessing_plan_path,
    eda_summary_path,
    eda_report_path,
]

minimum_required_plots = [
    plot_paths["eda_missingness_by_column"],
    plot_paths["eda_target_distribution_iri_mean_y"],
    plot_paths["eda_target_boxplot_iri_mean_y"],
    plot_paths["eda_numeric_correlation_heatmap"],
    plot_paths["eda_target_numeric_correlations"],
    plot_paths["eda_iri_mean_x_vs_iri_mean_y"],
]

missing_artifacts = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts if not p.exists()]
missing_plots = [str(p) for p in minimum_required_plots if not p.exists()]
if missing_artifacts or missing_plots:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN missing required outputs. "
        "Artifacts: " + "; ".join(missing_artifacts) +
        " Plots: " + "; ".join(missing_plots)
    )

# Reload and verify critical content.
with open(eda_plan_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    eda_plan_loaded = json.load(f)
if eda_plan_loaded.get("target_column") != TARGET_COLUMN:
    raise RuntimeError("eda_feature_engineering_plan.json target_column invalid.")
if eda_plan_loaded.get("task_type") != TASK_TYPE:
    raise RuntimeError("eda_feature_engineering_plan.json task_type invalid.")

with open(leakage_screening_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    leakage_screening_loaded = json.load(f)
if TARGET_COLUMN not in leakage_screening_loaded.get("final_excluded_columns", []):
    raise RuntimeError("leakage_screening_report.json does not exclude target.")

for feature_set_name, cols in feature_sets.items():
    if TARGET_COLUMN in cols:
        raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} found in feature set {feature_set_name}.")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
all_artifacts_saved = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts] + plot_paths_saved

mm.rag_add(
    kind="eda_feature_engineering_design",
    text=(
        "EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression. "

    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,

            "strict_no_prior_iri_count": len(strict_no_prior_iri),
            "feature_set_names": list(feature_sets.keys()),
        },
        "artifacts_saved": all_artifacts_saved,
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="feature_groups",
    text="Feature groups for pavement IRI_mean_y regression.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "feature_groups_path": str(feature_groups_path),
        "feature_group_names": list(feature_groups.keys()),
        "artifacts_saved": [str(feature_groups_path)],
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="leakage_screening",
    text="Leakage screening for IRI_mean_y regression features.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,

        "feature_set_names": list(feature_sets.keys()),

    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="eda_artifact",
    text="EDA artifacts and plots saved for IRI_mean_y regression.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "eda_summary_path": str(eda_summary_path),
        "eda_report_path": str(eda_report_path),
        "plot_paths": plot_paths_saved,
        "artifacts_saved": [str(eda_summary_path), str(eda_report_path)] + plot_paths_saved,
    }
)

# Verify mm records before pipeline_state.
eda_design_hits = rag_search(
    kind="eda_feature_engineering_design",
    query="EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)
feature_groups_hits = rag_search(
    kind="feature_groups",
    query="Feature groups for pavement IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)
leakage_screening_hits = rag_search(
    kind="leakage_screening",
    query="Leakage screening for IRI_mean_y regression features",
    k=10
)
eda_artifact_hits = rag_search(
    kind="eda_artifact",
    query="EDA artifacts and plots saved for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)

if not eda_design_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="eda_feature_engineering_design".')
if not feature_groups_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="feature_groups".')
if not leakage_screening_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="leakage_screening".')
if not eda_artifact_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="eda_artifact".')

mm.rag_add(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    text=(
        "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN. "
        "Leakage-controlled EDA and feature-engineering design completed for "
        "IRI_mean_y regression."

    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "artifacts_saved": all_artifacts_saved,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    }
)

pipeline_state_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN",
    k=10
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 11) Final hard gate.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
missing_gate_items = []
for p in required_artifacts:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(str(p))
for p in minimum_required_plots:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(str(p))
if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["baseline_condition_allowed"]:
    missing_gate_items.append("target leaked into baseline_condition_allowed")
if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["strict_no_prior_iri"]:
    missing_gate_items.append("target leaked into strict_no_prior_iri")
if not eda_design_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="eda_feature_engineering_design"')
if not feature_groups_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="feature_groups"')
if not leakage_screening_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="leakage_screening"')
if not eda_artifact_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="eda_artifact"')
if not pipeline_state_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append(
        'mm record kind="pipeline_state" with PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN'
    )

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN hard gate failed. Missing/invalid: "
        + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 12) Print reviewer gate evidence.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
gate_report = {
    "status": "EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN_COMPLETED_VERIFIED_PENDING_REVIEWER_APPROVAL",
    "DATASET_ID": DATASET_ID,
    "FILE_PATH": FILE_PATH,
    "file_exists": os.path.exists(FILE_PATH),
    "file_path_outside_WORK_DIR": True,
    "PINNED_FILE_PATH_matches_registry": (
        True if not pinned else str(Path(pinned).resolve()) == str(file_path_resolved)
    ),
    "state_already_completed_before_run": state_already_completed_before_run,
    "dataset_shape": [int(df.shape[0]), int(df.shape[1])],
    "dataset_columns_as_List_str": dataset_columns,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "approved_artifacts_used": [str(p) for p in approved_artifact_paths],
    "required_memory_retrieval_hit_counts": {
        "target_candidates_hits": len(target_candidates_memory_hits),
        "dataset_artifact_hits_for_COLUMNS_query": len(dataset_artifact_memory_hits),
        "model_metrics_hits_audit_only": len(model_metrics_memory_hits),
        "model_metrics_per_model_hits_audit_only": len(model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits),
        "discover_pipeline_state_hits": len(discover_pipeline_hits),
        "target_select_pipeline_state_hits": len(target_select_pipeline_hits),
        "target_selection_hits": len(target_selection_hits_prior),
        "leakage_policy_hits": len(leakage_policy_hits_prior),
    },
    "feature_group_artifact_paths": {
        "feature_groups_json": str(feature_groups_path),
        "eda_feature_engineering_plan_json": str(eda_plan_path),
    },
    "feature_audit_path": str(feature_audit_path),
    "leakage_screening_path": str(leakage_screening_path),
    "preprocessing_plan_path": str(preprocessing_plan_path),
    "eda_summary_path": str(eda_summary_path),
    "eda_report_path": str(eda_report_path),
    "plot_paths": plot_paths_saved,
    "feature_sets": {
        "baseline_condition_allowed": baseline_condition_allowed,
        "strict_no_prior_iri": strict_no_prior_iri,
    },
    "excluded_features": leakage_banned,
    "conditionally_allowed_features": conditionally_allowed_features,
    "availability_sensitive_features": availability_sensitive,
    "mm_record_confirmation": {
        "eda_feature_engineering_design_hits": len(eda_design_hits),
        "feature_groups_hits": len(feature_groups_hits),
        "leakage_screening_hits": len(leakage_screening_hits),
        "eda_artifact_hits": len(eda_artifact_hits),
        "pipeline_state_hits": len(pipeline_state_hits_after),
    },
    "pipeline_state_completed_text": "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN",
}

print(json.dumps(gate_report, indent=2))

plt.tight_layout()\\n            plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)\\n            plt.close()\\n            return\\n    save_placeholder_plot(path, title, f\\"{x_col} or {y_col} k=5\\n)\\nstate_already_completed_before_run = bool(eda_state_hits_before)\\n\\nmissing_upstream = []\\nfor p in approved_artifact_paths:\\n    if not p.exists():\\n \\"IRI_mean_x\\",\\n])\\n\\nnon_target_y_columns_found = [\\n    c for c in dataset_columns\\n    if c.endswith(\\"_y\\") and c != TARGET_COLUMN\\n]\\n\\ntarget_derived_patterns = [\\n    \\"target\\",\\n    \\"label\\",\\n    \\"outcome\\",\\n    \\"post\\",\\n    \\"future_condition\\",\\n    \\"delta_iri\\",\\n    \\"iri_change\\",\\n    \\"improvement\\",\\n    \\"deterioration\\",\\n]\\n\\npattern_excluded_columns = []\\nfor c in dataset_columns:\\n    cl = c.lower()\\n    if c == TARGET_COLUMN:\\n        continue\\n    if c in for k, v in df.dtypes.items()}\\nmissing_counts = df.isna().sum()\\nmissing_pcts = df.isna().mean() * 100.0\\n\\ngroup_membership = {}\\nfor group_name, cols in feature_groups.items():\\n    for col in cols:\\n        group_membership.setdefault(col, []).append(group_name)\\n\\naudit_rows = []\\nfor col in dataset_columns:\\n    groups = group_membership.get(col, [])\\n    if col == target; never include in X.\\"\\n    elif col in non_target_y_columns_found:\\n        role = \\"excluded_leakage\\"\\n        leakage_status = \\"non_target_y_column\\"\\n        action = \\"exclude_from_all_feature_sets\\"\\n        notes = \\"Non-target _y variable treated as potential outcome-side leakage.\\"\\n    elif col in pattern_excluded_columns:\\n        role = \\"conditionally_allowed_baseline_feature\\"\\n        action = \\"include in baseline_condition_allowed; exclude in strict_no_prior_iri\\"\\n        notes = \\"Allowed only if \\"availability_sensitive_not_auto_banned\\"\\n        action = \\"include with later availability sensitivity review\\"\\n        notes = \\"Traffic forecast may be available at planning time; do not sets\\"\\n        notes = \\"Candidate predictor subject to downstream preprocessing and train/CV-only fitting.\\"\\n    \\n    audit_rows.append({\\n        \\"column\\": col,\\n        \\"dtype\\": })\\n\\nfeature_audit_df = pd.DataFrame(audit_rows)\\nfeature_audit_df.to_csv(feature_audit_path, index=False)\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 5) \\"Urban_Type\\",\\n    \\"Surface_Type_mode\\",\\n    \\"Base_Type_mode_x\\",\\n    \\"Treatment_type\\",\\n])\\n\\nnumeric_columns = []\\nfor c in baseline_condition_allowed:\\n    if c not in },\\n        \\"combination_aadt_share\\": {\\n            \\"formula\\": \\"AADT_Combination_mean_x / AADT_mean_x\\",\\n            \\"safe_division\\": True,\\n            \\"required_columns\\": \\"task_type\\": TASK_TYPE,\\n    \\"numeric_columns\\": numeric_columns,\\n    \\"categorical_columns\\": categorical_columns,\\n    \\"numeric_imputation_strategy\\": \\"median imputation inside \\"engineered_feature_rules\\": engineered_feature_rules,\\n}\\n\\nleakage_screening = {\\n    \\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID,\\n    \\"file_path\\": FILE_PATH,\\n    \\"state\\": STATE_NOW,\\n    \\"target_column\\": TARGET_COLUMN,\\n    \\"banned_features\\": leakage_banned,\\n    \\"conditionally_allowed_features\\": conditionally_allowed_features,\\n    \\"availability_sensitive_features\\": availability_sensitive,\\n    \\"non_target_y_columns_found\\": non_target_y_columns_found,\\n    \\"target_derived_patterns_checked\\":   \\"Availability-sensitive traffic forecast; review whether it is available at prediction time.\\"\\n        ),\\n    },\\n}\\n\\neda_feature_engineering_plan = {\\n    \\"dataset_id\\": \\"task_type\\": TASK_TYPE,\\n    \\"feature_sets\\": feature_sets,\\n    \\"preprocessing_plan\\": preprocessing_plan,\\n    \\"feature_engineering_rules\\": engineered_feature_rules,\\n    \\"leakage_controls\\": leakage_screening,\\n    \\"no_model_training_in_this_state\\": True,\\n    \\"fit_final_preprocessors_only_inside_train_cv_folds\\": True,\\n}\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 6) EDA summaries.\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\nnumeric_df = pd.DataFrame(index=df.index)\\nfor c in dataset_columns:\\n    coerced = pd.to_numeric(df[c], errors=\\"coerce\\")\\n    if coerced.notna().sum() > 0:\\n        numeric_df[c] = coerced\\n\\nnumeric_summary = {}\\nfor c in numeric_df.columns:\\n    s = numeric_df[c]\\n    numeric_summary[c] = {\\n        \\"non_null_count\\": int(s.notna().sum()),\\n        \\"missing_count\\": int(s.isna().sum()),\\n        \\"mean\\": safe_float(s.mean(skipna=True)),\\n        \\"median\\": safe_float(s.median(skipna=True)),\\n        \\"std\\": safe_float(s.std(skipna=True)),\\n        \\"min\\": safe_float(s.min(skipna=True)),\\n        \\"max\\": safe_float(s.max(skipna=True)),\\n        \\"q25\\": safe_float(s.quantile(0.25)),\\n        \\"q75\\": safe_float(s.quantile(0.75)),\\n    }\\n\\ncategorical_summary = {}\\nfor c in categorical_columns:\\n    vc = df[c].astype(\\"object\\").where(df[c].notna(), \\"Unknown\\").astype(str).value_counts(dropna=False).head(20)\\n    categorical_summary[c] = {\\n        \\"unique_count\\": int(df[c].nunique(dropna=True)),\\n        \\"top_values\\": {str(k): int(v) for k, v in vc.items()},\\n    }\\n\\nmissingness_summary = [\\n    {\\n        \\"column\\": str(c),\\n        \\"missing_count\\": int(missing_counts[c]),\\n        \\"missing_percent\\": float(missing_pcts[c]),\\n    }\\n    for c in missing_counts.sort_values(ascending=False).index\\n]\\n\\ntarget_summary = {\\n    \\"target_column\\": TARGET_COLUMN,\\n    \\"task_type\\": TASK_TYPE,\\n    \\"dtype_original\\": str(df[TARGET_COLUMN].dtype),\\n    \\"non_null_count\\": int(target_numeric.notna().sum()),\\n    \\"missing_count\\": int(target_numeric.isna().sum()),\\n    \\"missing_percent\\": float(target_numeric.isna().mean() * 100.0),\\n    \\"min\\": safe_float(target_numeric.min(skipna=True)),\\n    \\"max\\": safe_float(target_numeric.max(skipna=True)),\\n    \\"mean\\": safe_float(target_numeric.mean(skipna=True)),\\n    \\"median\\": safe_float(target_numeric.median(skipna=True)),\\n    \\"standard_deviation\\": safe_float(target_numeric.std(skipna=True)),\\n    \\"quantiles\\": {\\n        \\"0.01\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.01)),\\n        \\"0.05\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.05)),\\n        \\"0.25\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.25)),\\n        \\"0.50\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.50)),\\n        \\"0.75\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.75)),\\n        \\"0.95\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.95)),\\n        \\"0.99\\": safe_float(target_numeric.quantile(0.99)),\\n    },\\n}\\n\\ncorrelation_with_target = {}\\nif TARGET_COLUMN in numeric_df.columns:\\n    for c in numeric_df.columns:\\n        if c == TARGET_COLUMN:\\n            continue\\n        valid = numeric_df[[c, TARGET_COLUMN]].dropna()\\n        if len(valid) >= 3:\\n            corr = valid[c].corr(valid[TARGET_COLUMN])\\n            correlation_with_target[c] = safe_float(corr)\\n\\ncorrelation_with_target_sorted = dict(\\n    sorted(\\n        correlation_with_target.items(),\\n        key=lambda kv: abs(kv[1]) if kv[1] is not None else -1,\\n        reverse=True\\n    )\\n)\\n\\noutlier_summary = {}\\nfor c in numeric_df.columns:\\n    s = numeric_df[c].dropna()\\n    if len(s) > 0:\\n        q1 = s.quantile(0.25)\\n        q3 = s.quantile(0.75)\\n        iqr = q3 - q1\\n        if pd.isna(iqr) or iqr == 0:\\n            lower = None\\n            upper = None\\n            outlier_count = 0\\n        else:\\n            lower = q1 - 1.5 * iqr\\n            upper = q3 + 1.5 * iqr\\n            outlier_count = int(((s < lower) | (s > upper)).sum())\\n        outlier_summary[c] = {\\n            \\"iqr_lower_fence\\": safe_float(lower),\\n            \\"iqr_upper_fence\\": safe_float(upper),\\n            \\"iqr_outlier_count\\": outlier_count,\\n            \\"iqr_outlier_percent_non_null\\": safe_float(outlier_count / len(s) * 100.0),\\n        }\\n\\neda_summary = {\\n    \\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID,\\n    \\"file_path\\": FILE_PATH,\\n    \\"state\\": STATE_NOW,\\n    \\"dataset_shape\\": {\\n        \\"rows\\": int(df.shape[0]),\\n        \\"columns\\": int(df.shape[1]),\\n    },\\n    \\"dataset_columns\\": dataset_columns,\\n    \\"target_summary\\": target_summary,\\n    \\"numeric_summary\\": numeric_summary,\\n    \\"categorical_summary\\": categorical_summary,\\n    \\"missingness_summary\\": missingness_summary,\\n    \\"correlation_with_target\\": correlation_with_target_sorted,\\n    \\"outlier_summary\\": outlier_summary,\\n}\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 7) Save JSON/CSV/Markdown artifacts.\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\nwith open(feature_groups_path, \\"w\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as f:\\n    json.dump({\\n json.dump(leakage_screening, f, indent=2)\\n\\nwith open(eda_plan_path, \\"w\\", encoding=\\"utf-8\\") as f:\\n    json.dump(eda_feature_engineering_plan, f, indent=2)\\n\\nwith open(eda_summary_path, ```python
mm.rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="target_candidates.csv",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

mm.rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="COLUMNS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
```

Also check upstream state completion:

```text

PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT
PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN
```

And check prior completion of:

```text

```

If already completed, do not blindly rerun unless planner requests rerun. Verify artifacts and only repair missing outputs.

---

# 4. Leakage controls to enforce

## Hard exclusions

Always exclude from `X`:

```text
IRI_mean_y
```

Also enforce exclusions from `leakage_screening_report.json`:

```text
final_excluded_columns
banned_features
non_target_y_columns_found
pattern_excluded_columns
```

If any feature set contains `IRI_mean_y`, fail fast.

## Feature sets

Use exactly the two approved feature-set concepts from EDA:

### A. `baseline_condition_allowed`

Includes `IRI_mean_x`.

Purpose:

- Measures best achievable predictive performance when baseline/current IRI is legitimately available before prediction.

### B. `strict_no_prior_iri`

Excludes `IRI_mean_x`.

Purpose:

- Deployment-safe or leakage-sensitivity comparison if temporal provenance of `IRI_mean_x` is uncertain.

## Explicit `IRI_mean_x` sensitivity analysis

The modeling report must compare best results between:

```text
baseline_condition_allowed
strict_no_prior_iri
```

and report:

```text
delta_RMSE
delta_MAE
delta_R2
```

Interpretation rule:

- If performance drops sharply when `IRI_mean_x` is removed, this does not automatically prove leakage, but it means the model is highly dependent on prior IRI and requires deployment-time availability confirmation.

---

# 5. Train/test split and cross-validation

Recommended:

```text
Holdout test size: 20%
Random seed: 42
CV folds: 5
```

For regression, use one of:

1. `KFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=42)`, or
2. target-binned stratified split for train/test only, if implemented carefully.

Preferred simple robust plan:

- Use `train_test_split(..., test_size=0.2, random_state=42)`.
- If feasible, stratify by quantile bins of `IRI_mean_y`; otherwise use non-stratified split and record reason.
- Use 5-fold CV on training data only for model selection / hyperparameter tuning.

Target handling:

- Drop rows with missing `IRI_mean_y`.
- Do not impute target.
- Coerce target to numeric and fail fast if non-null values cannot be coerced.

---

# 6. Preprocessing pipeline requirements

All preprocessing must be fitted inside sklearn pipelines and inside CV folds.

## Numeric features

- Coerce numeric columns with `pd.to_numeric(errors="coerce")` before pipeline construction or through a deterministic transformer.
- Impute numeric missing values with median.
- Scale numeric features for models requiring scaling.

Recommended numeric transformers:

For tree-based models:

```text
SimpleImputer(strategy="median")
```

For linear/kernel/distance-sensitive models:

```text
SimpleImputer(strategy="median")
StandardScaler()
```

## Categorical features

Expected categorical columns:

```text
F_System_mode
Urban_Type
Surface_Type_mode
Base_Type_mode_x
Treatment_type
```

Use:

```text
SimpleImputer(strategy="constant", fill_value="Unknown")
OneHotEncoder(handle_unknown="ignore")
```

## Engineered features

The EDA design proposed deterministic engineered features. For this modeling step, use either:

### Preferred conservative option

Start with approved raw feature sets only, then optionally add deterministic engineered features in a documented model variant.

### If engineered features are implemented

They must be created using feature-only columns and fitted/applied inside the pipeline or through deterministic pre-split transformation that does not use the target.

Allowed deterministic feature rules:

```text
single_unit_aadt_share = AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
combination_aadt_share = AADT_Combination_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
future_aadt_ratio = Future_AADT_mean_x / AADT_mean_x
total_structural_thickness = Thickness_Rigid_mean_x + Thickness_Flexible_mean_x + Base_Thickness_mean_x
has_rigid_thickness = indicator(Thickness_Rigid_mean_x > 0)
has_flexible_thickness = indicator(Thickness_Flexible_mean_x > 0)
freeze_precip_interaction = FRZ_IDX_x * PRECIPITATION_x
temperature_freeze_interaction = TEMP_AVG_x * FRZ_IDX_x
```

No engineered feature may use `IRI_mean_y`.

---

# 7. Candidate models

At least three advanced predictive regression models must be optimized and evaluated.

Recommended primary models:

## 1. Random Forest Regressor

```text
sklearn.ensemble.RandomForestRegressor
```

Strengths:

- Nonlinear relationships.
- Robust to mixed feature effects.
- Useful baseline ensemble.

Tune:

```text
n_estimators
max_depth
min_samples_split
min_samples_leaf
max_features
```

## 2. Extra Trees Regressor

```text
sklearn.ensemble.ExtraTreesRegressor
```

Strengths:

- Strong nonlinear tabular model.
- Often competitive with random forests.
- Useful for feature importance.

Tune:

```text
n_estimators
max_depth
min_samples_split
min_samples_leaf
max_features
```

## 3. Histogram Gradient Boosting Regressor

```text
sklearn.ensemble.HistGradientBoostingRegressor
```

Strengths:

- Gradient boosting for tabular data.
- Strong nonlinear performance.
- Available in sklearn.

Tune:

```text
learning_rate
max_iter
max_leaf_nodes
min_samples_leaf
l2_regularization
```

Optional additional models if available:

## 4. XGBoost Regressor

Use only if installed. Do not fail the whole pipeline if unavailable.

```text
xgboost.XGBRegressor
```

## 5. LightGBM Regressor

Use only if installed. Do not fail the whole pipeline if unavailable.

```text
lightgbm.LGBMRegressor
```

## 6. CatBoost Regressor

Use only if installed. Do not fail the whole pipeline if unavailable.

```text
catboost.CatBoostRegressor
```

Recommended governance:

- The state must complete with at least three successfully optimized models.
- Optional models may be skipped with a logged reason if package unavailable.

# 8. Hyperparameter optimization

Use one of:

```text
RandomizedSearchCV
HalvingRandomSearchCV
Optuna, if available and already installed
```

Recommended default:

```text
RandomizedSearchCV
```

Configuration:

```text
n_iter: 20 to 40 per model per feature set
cv: 5
scoring: neg_root_mean_squared_error
n_jobs: -1 where safe
random_state: 42
```

Because there are two feature sets, expect at minimum:

```text
3 models × 2 feature sets = 6 optimized model runs
```

The optimization should occur on the training split only.

After tuning, evaluate each best estimator on:

1. CV metrics from training data.
2. Final holdout test set.

---

# 9. Metrics

Compute and save multiple metrics.

Required regression metrics:

```text
RMSE
MAE
R2
Median Absolute Error
Explained Variance
MAPE or SMAPE
```

Use SMAPE if zero or near-zero targets may make MAPE unstable.

Recommended definitions:

```text
RMSE = sqrt(mean_squared_error)
MAE = mean_absolute_error
R2 = r2_score
MedAE = median_absolute_error
ExplainedVariance = explained_variance_score
SMAPE = mean(2 * abs(y_pred - y_true) / (abs(y_true) + abs(y_pred))) * 100
```

Also save:

```text
CV mean RMSE
CV std RMSE
CV mean MAE
CV mean R2
test RMSE
test MAE
test R2
```

Primary model selection criterion:

```text
lowest test RMSE
```

Secondary:

```text
lower MAE
higher R2
stable CV-to-test performance
```
