STATE_TASK: MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json
import math
import warnings
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

from joblib import dump

from sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split, KFold, RandomizedSearchCV, cross_validate
from sklearn.compose import ColumnTransformer
from sklearn.pipeline import Pipeline
from sklearn.preprocessing import OneHotEncoder, StandardScaler
from sklearn.impute import SimpleImputer
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor, ExtraTreesRegressor, HistGradientBoostingRegressor
from sklearn.metrics import (
    mean_squared_error,
    mean_absolute_error,
    r2_score,
    median_absolute_error,
    explained_variance_score,
)
from sklearn.inspection import permutation_importance

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")

WORK_DIR = "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace"
STATE_NOW = "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN"
TARGET_COLUMN = "IRI_mean_y"
TASK_TYPE = "regression"
RANDOM_STATE = 42
TEST_SIZE = 0.20
CV_FOLDS = 5

os.makedirs(WORK_DIR, exist_ok=True)
models_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "models"
predictions_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "predictions"
plots_dir = Path(WORK_DIR) / "plots"
models_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
predictions_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

# Fail-fast governance.
if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir_resolved = Path(WORK_DIR).resolve()
file_path_resolved = Path(FILE_PATH).resolve()

try:
    file_path_resolved.relative_to(work_dir_resolved)
    raise RuntimeError(f"Registry FILE_PATH must not be inside WORK_DIR: {FILE_PATH}")
except ValueError:
    pass

pinned = os.environ.get("PINNED_FILE_PATH")
if pinned and str(Path(pinned).resolve()) != str(file_path_resolved):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "PINNED_FILE_PATH does not match registry latest_path. "
        f"PINNED_FILE_PATH={pinned}; registry latest_path={FILE_PATH}"
    )

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

# Approved upstream artifacts.
approved_artifact_paths = {
    "eda_feature_engineering_plan": Path(WORK_DIR) / "eda_feature_engineering_plan.json",
    "feature_groups": Path(WORK_DIR) / "feature_groups.json",
    "feature_audit": Path(WORK_DIR) / "feature_audit.csv",
    "leakage_screening": Path(WORK_DIR) / "leakage_screening_report.json",
    "preprocessing_plan": Path(WORK_DIR) / "preprocessing_plan.json",
    "eda_summary": Path(WORK_DIR) / "eda_summary.json",
    "target_selection": Path(WORK_DIR) / "target_selection.json",
    "leakage_policy": Path(WORK_DIR) / "leakage_policy.json",
}

# Required modeling artifacts.
modeling_plan_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "modeling_plan.json"
modeling_feature_sets_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "modeling_feature_sets.json"
train_test_split_metadata_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "train_test_split_metadata.json"
model_performance_metrics_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "model_performance_metrics.csv"
model_metrics_json_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "model_metrics.json"
model_comparison_report_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "model_comparison_report.md"
feature_importance_best_models_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / "feature_importance_best_models.csv"
best_model_path = models_dir / "best_model.joblib"
best_model_metadata_path = models_dir / "best_model_metadata.json"

required_plot_paths = {
    "model_comparison_test_rmse": plots_dir / "model_comparison_test_rmse.png",
    "model_comparison_test_mae": plots_dir / "model_comparison_test_mae.png",
    "model_comparison_test_r2": plots_dir / "model_comparison_test_r2.png",
    "cv_rmse_by_model": plots_dir / "cv_rmse_by_model.png",
    "observed_vs_predicted_best_model": plots_dir / "observed_vs_predicted_best_model.png",
    "residuals_vs_predicted_best_model": plots_dir / "residuals_vs_predicted_best_model.png",
    "residual_distribution_best_model": plots_dir / "residual_distribution_best_model.png",
    "absolute_error_by_feature_set": plots_dir / "absolute_error_by_feature_set.png",
    "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison": plots_dir / "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison.png",
    "feature_importance_best_model": plots_dir / "feature_importance_best_model.png",
}

def rag_search(kind, query, k=10):
    return mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=k,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
    )

def safe_float(x):
    try:
        if x is None or pd.isna(x):
            return None
        return float(x)
    except Exception:
        return None

def sanitize_name(s):
    return "".join(ch if ch.isalnum() or ch in ["_", "-"] else "_" for ch in str(s))

def smape(y_true, y_pred):
    y_true = np.asarray(y_true, dtype=float)
    y_pred = np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=float)
    denom = np.abs(y_true) + np.abs(y_pred)
    mask = denom > 1e-12
    if not np.any(mask):
        return 0.0
    return float(np.mean(2.0 * np.abs(y_pred[mask] - y_true[mask]) / denom[mask]) * 100.0)

def rmse(y_true, y_pred):
    return float(np.sqrt(mean_squared_error(y_true, y_pred)))

def compute_metrics(y_true, y_pred):
    return {
        "rmse": rmse(y_true, y_pred),
        "mae": float(mean_absolute_error(y_true, y_pred)),
        "r2": float(r2_score(y_true, y_pred)),
        "median_absolute_error": float(median_absolute_error(y_true, y_pred)),
        "explained_variance": float(explained_variance_score(y_true, y_pred)),
        "smape": smape(y_true, y_pred),
    }

def target_summary(series):
    s = pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce")
    return {
        "count": int(s.notna().sum()),
        "missing_count": int(s.isna().sum()),
        "mean": safe_float(s.mean()),
        "median": safe_float(s.median()),
        "std": safe_float(s.std()),
        "min": safe_float(s.min()),
        "max": safe_float(s.max()),
        "q25": safe_float(s.quantile(0.25)),
        "q75": safe_float(s.quantile(0.75)),
    }

def make_ohe():
    try:
        return OneHotEncoder(handle_unknown="ignore", sparse_output=False)
    except TypeError:
        return OneHotEncoder(handle_unknown="ignore", sparse=False)

def build_preprocessor(feature_cols, categorical_expected, scale_numeric=False):
    categorical_cols = [c for c in feature_cols if c in categorical_expected]
    numeric_cols = [c for c in feature_cols if c not in categorical_cols]

    numeric_steps = [("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median"))]
    if scale_numeric:
        numeric_steps.append(("scaler", StandardScaler()))

    numeric_transformer = Pipeline(steps=numeric_steps)
    categorical_transformer = Pipeline(steps=[
        ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="constant", fill_value="Unknown")),
        ("onehot", make_ohe()),
    ])

    transformers = []
    if numeric_cols:
        transformers.append(("num", numeric_transformer, numeric_cols))
    if categorical_cols:
        transformers.append(("cat", categorical_transformer, categorical_cols))

    return ColumnTransformer(
        transformers=transformers,
        remainder="drop",
        verbose_feature_names_out=True,
        sparse_threshold=0.0,
    ), numeric_cols, categorical_cols

def plot_bar_metric(metrics_df, metric_col, path, title, ylabel):
    temp = metrics_df.copy()
    temp["label"] = temp["feature_set"] + " | " + temp["model_name"]
    temp = temp.sort_values(metric_col, ascending=True if metric_col != "test_r2" else False)
    plt.figure(figsize=(12, max(6, len(temp) * 0.45)))
    plt.barh(temp["label"], temp[metric_col])
    plt.xlabel(ylabel)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close()

def save_placeholder_plot(path, title, message):
    plt.figure(figsize=(9, 5))
    plt.text(0.5, 0.5, message, ha="center", va="center", wrap=True)
    plt.title(title)
    plt.axis("off")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(path, dpi=150)
    plt.close()

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1) Required mm retrievals and upstream state gates.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
target_candidates_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="target_candidates.csv",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
dataset_artifact_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="COLUMNS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
model_metrics_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

discover_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET",
    k=10
)
target_select_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
    k=10
)
eda_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN",
    k=10
)
modeling_state_hits_before = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN",
    k=5
)
state_already_completed_before_run = bool(modeling_state_hits_before)

missing_upstream = []
for name, p in approved_artifact_paths.items():
    if not p.exists():
        missing_upstream.append(f"{name}: {p}")
if not discover_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET")
if not target_select_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT")
if not eda_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN")

if missing_upstream:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Cannot run MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN because upstream gates are incomplete. "
        "Missing: " + "; ".join(missing_upstream)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2) Load upstream artifacts and pinned dataset.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
with open(approved_artifact_paths["eda_feature_engineering_plan"], "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    eda_plan = json.load(f)
with open(approved_artifact_paths["leakage_screening"], "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    leakage_screening = json.load(f)
with open(approved_artifact_paths["preprocessing_plan"], "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    preprocessing_plan_upstream = json.load(f)
with open(approved_artifact_paths["target_selection"], "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    target_selection = json.load(f)
with open(approved_artifact_paths["leakage_policy"], "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    leakage_policy = json.load(f)

if target_selection.get("target_column") != TARGET_COLUMN:
    raise RuntimeError(f"target_selection target mismatch: {target_selection.get('target_column')}")
if target_selection.get("task_type") != TASK_TYPE:
    raise RuntimeError(f"target_selection task_type mismatch: {target_selection.get('task_type')}")
if TARGET_COLUMN not in leakage_policy.get("banned_features", []):
    raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} is not present in leakage_policy banned_features.")
if eda_plan.get("target_column") != TARGET_COLUMN or eda_plan.get("task_type") != TASK_TYPE:
    raise RuntimeError("EDA plan target/task mismatch.")

df = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False)
dataset_columns = [str(c) for c in df.columns.tolist()]
df.columns = dataset_columns

if TARGET_COLUMN not in dataset_columns:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Dataset missing target column {TARGET_COLUMN}.")

target_raw = df[TARGET_COLUMN]
target_numeric = pd.to_numeric(target_raw, errors="coerce")
coercion_fail_count = int(target_raw.notna().sum() - target_numeric.notna().sum())
if coercion_fail_count > 0:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Target {TARGET_COLUMN} has {coercion_fail_count} non-null values not coercible to numeric."
    )
if int(target_numeric.notna().sum()) == 0:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Target {TARGET_COLUMN} has no usable numeric values.")

df_model = df.copy()
df_model[TARGET_COLUMN] = target_numeric
df_model = df_model.loc[df_model[TARGET_COLUMN].notna()].copy()
df_model["__row_id__"] = df_model.index.astype(int)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3) Enforce leakage-controlled feature sets.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
feature_sets_from_eda = eda_plan.get("feature_sets", {})
baseline_condition_allowed = list(feature_sets_from_eda.get("baseline_condition_allowed", []))
strict_no_prior_iri = list(feature_sets_from_eda.get("strict_no_prior_iri", []))

if not baseline_condition_allowed or not strict_no_prior_iri:
    raise RuntimeError("EDA feature sets missing or empty.")

final_excluded_columns = set(leakage_screening.get("final_excluded_columns", []))
banned_features = set(leakage_screening.get("banned_features", []))
non_target_y_columns = set(leakage_screening.get("non_target_y_columns_found", []))
pattern_excluded_columns = set(leakage_screening.get("pattern_excluded_columns", []))
all_excluded = final_excluded_columns | banned_features | non_target_y_columns | pattern_excluded_columns | {TARGET_COLUMN}

def validate_feature_set(name, cols):
    missing = [c for c in cols if c not in dataset_columns]
    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Feature set {name} contains columns not in dataset: {missing}")
    leaked = [c for c in cols if c in all_excluded]
    if leaked:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Feature set {name} contains leakage/excluded columns: {leaked}")
    if TARGET_COLUMN in cols:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Feature set {name} contains target {TARGET_COLUMN}.")

validate_feature_set("baseline_condition_allowed", baseline_condition_allowed)
validate_feature_set("strict_no_prior_iri", strict_no_prior_iri)

if "IRI_mean_x" not in baseline_condition_allowed:
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x must be included in baseline_condition_allowed.")
if "IRI_mean_x" in strict_no_prior_iri:
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x must be excluded from strict_no_prior_iri.")

feature_sets = {
    "baseline_condition_allowed": baseline_condition_allowed,
    "strict_no_prior_iri": strict_no_prior_iri,
}

categorical_expected = [
    c for c in preprocessing_plan_upstream.get("categorical_columns", [])
    if c in dataset_columns
]
if not categorical_expected:
    categorical_expected = [
        c for c in ["F_System_mode", "Urban_Type", "Surface_Type_mode", "Base_Type_mode_x", "Treatment_type"]
        if c in dataset_columns
    ]

# Deterministic feature-only type coercion before split:
# numeric columns are coerced; categoricals are strings. No fitting and no target use.
all_model_features = sorted(set(baseline_condition_allowed + strict_no_prior_iri))
for c in all_model_features:
    if c in categorical_expected:
        df_model[c] = df_model[c].astype("object").where(df_model[c].notna(), np.nan)
    else:
        df_model[c] = pd.to_numeric(df_model[c], errors="coerce")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4) Train/test split with optional target quantile stratification.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
y_all = df_model[TARGET_COLUMN].astype(float)
row_ids_all = df_model["__row_id__"].astype(int)

stratification_used = False
stratification_notes = "Non-stratified split used."
stratify_values = None

try:
    bins = pd.qcut(y_all, q=10, duplicates="drop")
    bin_counts = bins.value_counts()
    if len(bin_counts) >= 2 and int(bin_counts.min()) >= 2:
        stratify_values = bins.astype(str)
        stratification_used = True
        stratification_notes = "Target quantile-bin stratification used for train/test split."
except Exception as exc:
    stratification_notes = f"Target quantile stratification not used: {repr(exc)}"

indices = np.arange(len(df_model))
train_idx, test_idx = train_test_split(
    indices,
    test_size=TEST_SIZE,
    random_state=RANDOM_STATE,
    stratify=stratify_values if stratification_used else None,
)

train_row_ids = row_ids_all.iloc[train_idx].tolist()
test_row_ids = row_ids_all.iloc[test_idx].tolist()
y_train = y_all.iloc[train_idx]
y_test = y_all.iloc[test_idx]

train_test_split_metadata = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "random_state": RANDOM_STATE,
    "test_size": TEST_SIZE,
    "train_row_count": int(len(train_idx)),
    "test_row_count": int(len(test_idx)),
    "target_summary_train": target_summary(y_train),
    "target_summary_test": target_summary(y_test),
    "stratification_used": bool(stratification_used),
    "stratification_notes": stratification_notes,
}
with open(train_test_split_metadata_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(train_test_split_metadata, f, indent=2)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5) Define models and hyperparameter searches.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
cv = KFold(n_splits=CV_FOLDS, shuffle=True, random_state=RANDOM_STATE)

model_specs = {
    "RandomForestRegressor": {
        "estimator": RandomForestRegressor(random_state=RANDOM_STATE, n_jobs=-1),
        "scale_numeric": False,
        "params": {
            "model__n_estimators": [120, 200, 300],
            "model__max_depth": [None, 8, 12, 18, 25],
            "model__min_samples_split": [2, 5, 10],
            "model__min_samples_leaf": [1, 2, 4],
            "model__max_features": ["sqrt", 0.6, 0.8, 1.0],
        },
    },
    "ExtraTreesRegressor": {
        "estimator": ExtraTreesRegressor(random_state=RANDOM_STATE, n_jobs=-1),
        "scale_numeric": False,
        "params": {
            "model__n_estimators": [120, 200, 300],
            "model__max_depth": [None, 8, 12, 18, 25],
            "model__min_samples_split": [2, 5, 10],
            "model__min_samples_leaf": [1, 2, 4],
            "model__max_features": ["sqrt", 0.6, 0.8, 1.0],
        },
    },
    "HistGradientBoostingRegressor": {
        "estimator": HistGradientBoostingRegressor(random_state=RANDOM_STATE),
        "scale_numeric": False,
        "params": {
            "model__learning_rate": [0.03, 0.05, 0.08, 0.10],
            "model__max_iter": [120, 200, 300],
            "model__max_leaf_nodes": [15, 31, 63],
            "model__min_samples_leaf": [10, 20, 35, 50],
            "model__l2_regularization": [0.0, 0.01, 0.1, 1.0],
        },
    },
}

N_ITER = 10

metrics_rows = []
prediction_artifacts = []
trained_model_artifacts = []
feature_importance_artifacts = []
skipped_models = []
fitted_results = {}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6) Train, tune, evaluate each model × feature set.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
for feature_set_name, feature_cols in feature_sets.items():
    X_train = df_model.iloc[train_idx][feature_cols].copy()
    X_test = df_model.iloc[test_idx][feature_cols].copy()

    for model_name, spec in model_specs.items():
        safe_model_name = sanitize_name(model_name)
        safe_feature_set = sanitize_name(feature_set_name)
        model_artifact_path = models_dir / f"model__{safe_feature_set}__{safe_model_name}.joblib"
        prediction_artifact_path = predictions_dir / f"predictions__{safe_feature_set}__{safe_model_name}.csv"

        try:
            preprocessor, numeric_cols_used, categorical_cols_used = build_preprocessor(
                feature_cols=feature_cols,
                categorical_expected=categorical_expected,
                scale_numeric=bool(spec.get("scale_numeric", False)),
            )

            pipeline = Pipeline(steps=[
                ("preprocessor", preprocessor),
                ("model", spec["estimator"]),
            ])

            search = RandomizedSearchCV(
                estimator=pipeline,
                param_distributions=spec["params"],
                n_iter=N_ITER,
                scoring="neg_root_mean_squared_error",
                cv=cv,
                random_state=RANDOM_STATE,
                n_jobs=-1,
                refit=True,
                return_train_score=False,
                error_score="raise",
            )

            search.fit(X_train, y_train)
            best_estimator = search.best_estimator_

            cv_scores = cross_validate(
                best_estimator,
                X_train,
                y_train,
                cv=cv,
                scoring={
                    "rmse": "neg_root_mean_squared_error",
                    "mae": "neg_mean_absolute_error",
                    "r2": "r2",
                },
                n_jobs=-1,
                return_train_score=False,
            )

            y_pred = best_estimator.predict(X_test)
            test_metrics = compute_metrics(y_test, y_pred)

            pred_df = pd.DataFrame({
                "row_id": test_row_ids,
                "feature_set": feature_set_name,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "y_true": y_test.to_numpy(dtype=float),
                "y_pred": np.asarray(y_pred, dtype=float),
            })
            pred_df["residual"] = pred_df["y_true"] - pred_df["y_pred"]
            pred_df["absolute_error"] = pred_df["residual"].abs()
            pred_df["squared_error"] = pred_df["residual"] ** 2
            pred_df.to_csv(prediction_artifact_path, index=False)

            dump(best_estimator, model_artifact_path)

            metrics_row = {
                "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
                "feature_set": feature_set_name,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "cv_rmse_mean": float(-np.mean(cv_scores["test_rmse"])),
                "cv_rmse_std": float(np.std(-cv_scores["test_rmse"])),
                "cv_mae_mean": float(-np.mean(cv_scores["test_mae"])),
                "cv_r2_mean": float(np.mean(cv_scores["test_r2"])),
                "test_rmse": test_metrics["rmse"],
                "test_mae": test_metrics["mae"],
                "test_r2": test_metrics["r2"],
                "test_median_absolute_error": test_metrics["median_absolute_error"],
                "test_explained_variance": test_metrics["explained_variance"],
                "test_smape": test_metrics["smape"],
                "best_params": json.dumps(search.best_params_, default=str),
                "model_artifact_path": str(model_artifact_path),
                "prediction_artifact_path": str(prediction_artifact_path),
                "numeric_columns_used": json.dumps(numeric_cols_used),
                "categorical_columns_used": json.dumps(categorical_cols_used),
            }
            metrics_rows.append(metrics_row)
            prediction_artifacts.append(str(prediction_artifact_path))
            trained_model_artifacts.append(str(model_artifact_path))

            fitted_results[(feature_set_name, model_name)] = {
                "estimator": best_estimator,
                "X_test": X_test,
                "y_test": y_test.copy(),
                "y_pred": np.asarray(y_pred),
                "metrics": metrics_row,
                "feature_cols": feature_cols,
                "prediction_path": str(prediction_artifact_path),
                "model_path": str(model_artifact_path),
            }

            # Optional native transformed feature importance where supported.
            final_model = best_estimator.named_steps["model"]
            if hasattr(final_model, "feature_importances_"):
                try:
                    transformed_names = best_estimator.named_steps["preprocessor"].get_feature_names_out()
                    importances = final_model.feature_importances_
                    if len(transformed_names) == len(importances):
                        fi_df = pd.DataFrame({
                            "feature_set": feature_set_name,
                            "model_name": model_name,
                            "feature": transformed_names,
                            "importance": importances,
                            "importance_type": "native_transformed_feature_importance",
                        }).sort_values("importance", ascending=False)
                        fi_path = Path(WORK_DIR) / f"feature_importance__{safe_feature_set}__{safe_model_name}.csv"
                        fi_df.to_csv(fi_path, index=False)
                        feature_importance_artifacts.append(str(fi_path))
                except Exception:
                    pass

        except Exception as exc:
            skipped_models.append({
                "feature_set": feature_set_name,
                "model_name": model_name,
                "reason": repr(exc),
            })

metrics_df = pd.DataFrame(metrics_rows)

required_metric_columns = [
    "dataset_id",
    "feature_set",
    "model_name",
    "cv_rmse_mean",
    "cv_rmse_std",
    "cv_mae_mean",
    "cv_r2_mean",
    "test_rmse",
    "test_mae",
    "test_r2",
    "test_median_absolute_error",
    "test_explained_variance",
    "test_smape",
    "best_params",
    "model_artifact_path",
    "prediction_artifact_path",
]

if metrics_df.empty:
    raise RuntimeError(f"No models completed successfully. Skipped: {skipped_models}")

successful_model_names = sorted(metrics_df["model_name"].unique().tolist())
if len(successful_model_names) < 3:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "At least three advanced regression model types must complete successfully. "
        f"Completed: {successful_model_names}; skipped={skipped_models}"
    )

expected_runs = set((fs, mn) for fs in feature_sets for mn in model_specs)
completed_runs = set(zip(metrics_df["feature_set"], metrics_df["model_name"]))
missing_runs = sorted(expected_runs - completed_runs)
if missing_runs:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "All required model × feature-set runs must complete. Missing: "
        + json.dumps(missing_runs)
    )

metrics_df = metrics_df.sort_values(["test_rmse", "test_mae", "feature_set", "model_name"]).reset_index(drop=True)
metrics_df.to_csv(model_performance_metrics_path, index=False)

missing_metric_columns = [c for c in required_metric_columns if c not in metrics_df.columns]
if missing_metric_columns:
    raise RuntimeError("model_performance_metrics.csv missing columns: " + "; ".join(missing_metric_columns))

best_row = metrics_df.iloc[0].to_dict()
best_feature_set = str(best_row["feature_set"])
best_model_name = str(best_row["model_name"])
best_key = (best_feature_set, best_model_name)
best_result = fitted_results[best_key]

dump(best_result["estimator"], best_model_path)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7) IRI_mean_x sensitivity analysis.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
best_by_feature_set = {}
for fs in feature_sets:
    sub = metrics_df[metrics_df["feature_set"] == fs].sort_values("test_rmse")
    if len(sub) == 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"No metrics for feature set {fs}")
    best_by_feature_set[fs] = sub.iloc[0].to_dict()

base_best = best_by_feature_set["baseline_condition_allowed"]
strict_best = best_by_feature_set["strict_no_prior_iri"]

iri_sensitivity = {
    "baseline_condition_allowed_best_model": base_best["model_name"],
    "strict_no_prior_iri_best_model": strict_best["model_name"],
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_rmse": safe_float(base_best["test_rmse"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_rmse": safe_float(strict_best["test_rmse"]),
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_mae": safe_float(base_best["test_mae"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_mae": safe_float(strict_best["test_mae"]),
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_r2": safe_float(base_best["test_r2"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_r2": safe_float(strict_best["test_r2"]),
    "delta_RMSE_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_rmse"] - base_best["test_rmse"]),
    "delta_MAE_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_mae"] - base_best["test_mae"]),
    "delta_R2_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_r2"] - base_best["test_r2"]),
    "interpretation": (
        "A performance drop when IRI_mean_x is removed does not automatically prove leakage, "
        "but indicates dependence on prior IRI and requires deployment-time availability confirmation."
    ),
}

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8) Feature importance for best models per feature set.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
best_importance_rows = []
for fs, fs_best in best_by_feature_set.items():
    mn = fs_best["model_name"]
    result = fitted_results[(fs, mn)]
    estimator = result["estimator"]
    X_test_fs = result["X_test"]
    y_test_fs = result["y_test"]

    try:
        perm = permutation_importance(
            estimator,
            X_test_fs,
            y_test_fs,
            n_repeats=5,
            random_state=RANDOM_STATE,
            scoring="neg_root_mean_squared_error",
            n_jobs=-1,
        )
        for col, mean_imp, std_imp in zip(X_test_fs.columns, perm.importances_mean, perm.importances_std):
            best_importance_rows.append({
                "feature_set": fs,
                "model_name": mn,
                "feature": col,
                "importance": float(mean_imp),
                "importance_std": float(std_imp),
                "importance_type": "permutation_importance_neg_rmse_on_test",
            })
    except Exception as exc:
        best_importance_rows.append({
            "feature_set": fs,
            "model_name": mn,
            "feature": "__PERMUTATION_IMPORTANCE_FAILED__",
            "importance": np.nan,
            "importance_std": np.nan,
            "importance_type": f"failed: {repr(exc)}",
        })

feature_importance_best_models_df = pd.DataFrame(best_importance_rows)
feature_importance_best_models_df = feature_importance_best_models_df.sort_values(
    ["feature_set", "importance"],
    ascending=[True, False],
    na_position="last"
)
feature_importance_best_models_df.to_csv(feature_importance_best_models_path, index=False)
feature_importance_artifacts.append(str(feature_importance_best_models_path))

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 9) Save modeling plans, feature sets, metrics JSON, best metadata.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
modeling_feature_sets = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "feature_sets": feature_sets,
    "leakage_checks": {
        "IRI_mean_y_excluded_from_all_feature_sets": all(TARGET_COLUMN not in cols for cols in feature_sets.values()),
        "IRI_mean_x_included_only_in_baseline_condition_allowed": (
            "IRI_mean_x" in feature_sets["baseline_condition_allowed"]
            and "IRI_mean_x" not in feature_sets["strict_no_prior_iri"]
        ),
        "final_excluded_columns": sorted(all_excluded),
    }
}
with open(modeling_feature_sets_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(modeling_feature_sets, f, indent=2)

modeling_plan = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "created_at_utc": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + "Z",
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "feature_sets": list(feature_sets.keys()),
    "models": list(model_specs.keys()),
    "cv_strategy": {
        "type": "KFold",
        "n_splits": CV_FOLDS,
        "shuffle": True,
        "random_state": RANDOM_STATE,
        "used_on": "training split only",
    },
    "test_split_strategy": {
        "test_size": TEST_SIZE,
        "random_state": RANDOM_STATE,
        "stratification_used": stratification_used,
        "stratification_notes": stratification_notes,
    },
    "hyperparameter_optimization": {
        "method": "RandomizedSearchCV",
        "n_iter_per_model_per_feature_set": N_ITER,
        "scoring": "neg_root_mean_squared_error",
        "refit": True,
    },
    "preprocessing_governance": (
        "All imputers, encoders, and model preprocessing are inside sklearn pipelines "
        "and fitted within train/CV folds. No preprocessing is fit globally for evaluation."
    ),
    "leakage_controls": {
        "excluded_features": sorted(all_excluded),
        "IRI_mean_y_excluded_from_X": True,
        "IRI_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison": "baseline_condition_allowed vs strict_no_prior_iri",
    },
}
with open(modeling_plan_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(modeling_plan, f, indent=2)

model_metrics_json = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "primary_selection_metric": "lowest test_rmse",
    "metrics_path": str(model_performance_metrics_path),
    "all_results": metrics_df[required_metric_columns].to_dict(orient="records"),
    "best_model": {
        "feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "model_name": best_model_name,
        "test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "model_artifact_path": str(best_model_path),
        "source_model_artifact_path": best_row["model_artifact_path"],
        "prediction_artifact_path": best_row["prediction_artifact_path"],
    },
    "best_by_feature_set": best_by_feature_set,
    "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_summary": iri_sensitivity,
    "skipped_models": skipped_models,
}
with open(model_metrics_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(model_metrics_json, f, indent=2)

best_model_metadata = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "best_model_name": best_model_name,
    "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
    "best_model_path": str(best_model_path),
    "source_model_artifact_path": best_row["model_artifact_path"],
    "feature_columns": feature_sets[best_feature_set],
    "metrics": {
        "test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "test_median_absolute_error": safe_float(best_row["test_median_absolute_error"]),
        "test_explained_variance": safe_float(best_row["test_explained_variance"]),
        "test_smape": safe_float(best_row["test_smape"]),
    },
    "selection_rule": "lowest test RMSE, with leakage/deployment sensitivity documented",
}
with open(best_model_metadata_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(best_model_metadata, f, indent=2)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 10) Diagnostic plots.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
plot_bar_metric(metrics_df, "test_rmse", required_plot_paths["model_comparison_test_rmse"], "Model Comparison: Test RMSE", "Test RMSE")
plot_bar_metric(metrics_df, "test_mae", required_plot_paths["model_comparison_test_mae"], "Model Comparison: Test MAE", "Test MAE")
plot_bar_metric(metrics_df, "test_r2", required_plot_paths["model_comparison_test_r2"], "Model Comparison: Test R²", "Test R²")
plot_bar_metric(metrics_df, "cv_rmse_mean", required_plot_paths["cv_rmse_by_model"], "Cross-Validation RMSE by Model", "CV RMSE mean")

best_pred_df = pd.read_csv(best_row["prediction_artifact_path"])

plt.figure(figsize=(7, 7))
plt.scatter(best_pred_df["y_true"], best_pred_df["y_pred"], alpha=0.45, s=16)
lims = [
    float(min(best_pred_df["y_true"].min(), best_pred_df["y_pred"].min())),
    float(max(best_pred_df["y_true"].max(), best_pred_df["y_pred"].max())),
]
plt.plot(lims, lims, "r--", linewidth=1)
plt.xlabel("Observed IRI_mean_y")
plt.ylabel("Predicted IRI_mean_y")
plt.title(f"Observed vs Predicted: {best_model_name} ({best_feature_set})")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["observed_vs_predicted_best_model"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

plt.figure(figsize=(8, 6))
plt.scatter(best_pred_df["y_pred"], best_pred_df["residual"], alpha=0.45, s=16)
plt.axhline(0, color="red", linestyle="--", linewidth=1)
plt.xlabel("Predicted IRI_mean_y")
plt.ylabel("Residual (y_true - y_pred)")
plt.title(f"Residuals vs Predicted: {best_model_name} ({best_feature_set})")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["residuals_vs_predicted_best_model"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

plt.figure(figsize=(8, 6))
plt.hist(best_pred_df["residual"], bins=40, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
plt.xlabel("Residual")
plt.ylabel("Count")
plt.title(f"Residual Distribution: {best_model_name} ({best_feature_set})")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["residual_distribution_best_model"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

abs_err_by_fs = []
for fs in feature_sets:
    fs_best = best_by_feature_set[fs]
    p = pd.read_csv(fs_best["prediction_artifact_path"])
    abs_err_by_fs.append((fs, p["absolute_error"].values))
plt.figure(figsize=(8, 6))
plt.boxplot([x[1] for x in abs_err_by_fs], labels=[x[0] for x in abs_err_by_fs], showfliers=False)
plt.ylabel("Absolute Error")
plt.title("Absolute Error by Feature Set - Best Model per Feature Set")
plt.xticks(rotation=20, ha="right")
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["absolute_error_by_feature_set"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

sens_metrics = ["test_rmse", "test_mae", "test_r2"]
x = np.arange(len(sens_metrics))
base_vals = [base_best[m] for m in sens_metrics]
strict_vals = [strict_best[m] for m in sens_metrics]
width = 0.35
plt.figure(figsize=(9, 6))
plt.bar(x - width/2, base_vals, width, label="baseline_condition_allowed")
plt.bar(x + width/2, strict_vals, width, label="strict_no_prior_iri")
plt.xticks(x, ["RMSE", "MAE", "R²"])
plt.title("IRI_mean_x Sensitivity Comparison")
plt.legend()
plt.tight_layout()
plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["iri_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison"], dpi=150)
plt.close()

best_fi = feature_importance_best_models_df[
    (feature_importance_best_models_df["feature_set"] == best_feature_set)
    & (feature_importance_best_models_df["model_name"] == best_model_name)
    & (feature_importance_best_models_df["feature"] != "__PERMUTATION_IMPORTANCE_FAILED__")
].copy()
if len(best_fi) > 0:
    best_fi = best_fi.sort_values("importance", ascending=True).tail(20)
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, max(6, len(best_fi) * 0.35)))
    plt.barh(best_fi["feature"], best_fi["importance"])
    plt.xlabel("Permutation importance (increase in neg RMSE score)")
    plt.title(f"Feature Importance: Best Model {best_model_name}")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(required_plot_paths["feature_importance_best_model"], dpi=150)
    plt.close()
else:
    save_placeholder_plot(
        required_plot_paths["feature_importance_best_model"],
        "Feature Importance Best Model",
        "Permutation importance unavailable."
    )

# Optional per-model diagnostic plots.
extra_plot_paths = []
for (fs, mn), result in fitted_results.items():
    safe_fs = sanitize_name(fs)
    safe_mn = sanitize_name(mn)
    pred_path = result["prediction_path"]
    p = pd.read_csv(pred_path)

    obs_plot = plots_dir / f"observed_vs_predicted__{safe_fs}__{safe_mn}.png"
    resid_dist_plot = plots_dir / f"residual_distribution__{safe_fs}__{safe_mn}.png"
    resid_pred_plot = plots_dir / f"residuals_vs_predicted__{safe_fs}__{safe_mn}.png"

    plt.figure(figsize=(6, 6))
    plt.scatter(p["y_true"], p["y_pred"], alpha=0.35, s=12)
    lims = [float(min(p["y_true"].min(), p["y_pred"].min())), float(max(p["y_true"].max(), p["y_pred"].max()))]
    plt.plot(lims, lims, "r--", linewidth=1)
    plt.xlabel("Observed")
    plt.ylabel("Predicted")
    plt.title(f"Observed vs Predicted: {mn} | {fs}")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(obs_plot, dpi=120)
    plt.close()

    plt.figure(figsize=(7, 5))
    plt.hist(p["residual"], bins=35, edgecolor="black", alpha=0.75)
    plt.xlabel("Residual")
    plt.title(f"Residual Distribution: {mn} | {fs}")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(resid_dist_plot, dpi=120)
    plt.close()

    plt.figure(figsize=(7, 5))
    plt.scatter(p["y_pred"], p["residual"], alpha=0.35, s=12)
    plt.axhline(0, color="red", linestyle="--", linewidth=1)
    plt.xlabel("Predicted")
    plt.ylabel("Residual")
    plt.title(f"Residuals vs Predicted: {mn} | {fs}")
    plt.tight_layout()
    plt.savefig(resid_pred_plot, dpi=120)
    plt.close()

    extra_plot_paths.extend([str(obs_plot), str(resid_dist_plot), str(resid_pred_plot)])

required_plot_paths_saved = [str(p) for p in required_plot_paths.values()]
all_plot_paths_saved = required_plot_paths_saved + extra_plot_paths

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 11) Human-readable model comparison report.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
metrics_markdown = metrics_df[required_metric_columns].to_markdown(index=False)
top_fi_markdown = best_fi.sort_values("importance", ascending=False).head(15).to_markdown(index=False) if len(best_fi) > 0 else "Feature importance unavailable."

report_md = f"""# MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN Report

## Target and Task

- Target: `{TARGET_COLUMN}`
- Task type: `{TASK_TYPE}`
- Dataset ID: `{DATASET_ID}`
- Dataset shape used after dropping missing target rows: `{df_model.shape[0]}` rows x `{df_model.shape[1]}` columns

## Leakage Controls

- `{TARGET_COLUMN}` was excluded from every feature matrix.
- `baseline_condition_allowed` includes `IRI_mean_x`.
- `strict_no_prior_iri` excludes `IRI_mean_x`.
- Final excluded columns:

```json
{json.dumps(sorted(all_excluded), indent=2)}
```

## Train/Test and CV Strategy

- Holdout test size: `{TEST_SIZE}`
- Random seed: `{RANDOM_STATE}`
- CV: `{CV_FOLDS}`-fold shuffled KFold on training data only
- Stratification used: `{stratification_used}`
- Stratification notes: `{stratification_notes}`

All preprocessing was implemented inside sklearn pipelines. Imputers and encoders were fit only inside train/CV folds during model selection and on the training split before final holdout evaluation.

## Models Optimized

```json
{json.dumps(list(model_specs.keys()), indent=2)}
```

Hyperparameter optimization used `RandomizedSearchCV` with `{N_ITER}` sampled configurations per model per feature set, optimizing negative RMSE.

## Model Performance

{metrics_markdown}

## Best Model

```json
{json.dumps(model_metrics_json["best_model"], indent=2)}
```

## IRI_mean_x Sensitivity

```json
{json.dumps(iri_sensitivity, indent=2)}
```

A performance drop when `IRI_mean_x` is removed does not automatically prove leakage, but it indicates reliance on prior IRI and requires confirmation that baseline IRI is available at prediction time.

## Best Model Feature Importance

{top_fi_markdown}

## Saved Artifacts

- Modeling plan: `{modeling_plan_path}`
- Modeling feature sets: `{modeling_feature_sets_path}`
- Train/test split metadata: `{train_test_split_metadata_path}`
- Model performance metrics: `{model_performance_metrics_path}`
- Model metrics JSON: `{model_metrics_json_path}`
- Best model: `{best_model_path}`
- Best model metadata: `{best_model_metadata_path}`
- Predictions directory: `{predictions_dir}`
- Models directory: `{models_dir}`
- Plots directory: `{plots_dir}`
"""

with open(model_comparison_report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(report_md)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 12) Verify required files/artifacts.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
required_artifacts = [
    modeling_plan_path,
    modeling_feature_sets_path,
    train_test_split_metadata_path,
    model_performance_metrics_path,
    model_metrics_json_path,
    model_comparison_report_path,
    feature_importance_best_models_path,
    best_model_path,
    best_model_metadata_path,
]
missing_artifacts = [str(p) for p in required_artifacts if not p.exists()]
missing_plots = [str(p) for p in required_plot_paths.values() if not p.exists()]
missing_model_artifacts = [p for p in trained_model_artifacts if not Path(p).exists()]
missing_prediction_artifacts = [p for p in prediction_artifacts if not Path(p).exists()]

if missing_artifacts or missing_plots or missing_model_artifacts or missing_prediction_artifacts:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN required outputs missing. "
        f"artifacts={missing_artifacts}; plots={missing_plots}; "
        f"models={missing_model_artifacts}; predictions={missing_prediction_artifacts}"
    )

# Reload checks.
check_metrics = pd.read_csv(model_performance_metrics_path)
for c in required_metric_columns:
    if c not in check_metrics.columns:
        raise RuntimeError(f"model_performance_metrics.csv missing required column {c}")

for pred_path in prediction_artifacts:
    pred_cols = list(pd.read_csv(pred_path, nrows=1).columns)
    expected_pred_cols = [
        "row_id", "feature_set", "model_name", "y_true", "y_pred",
        "residual", "absolute_error", "squared_error"
    ]
    if pred_cols != expected_pred_cols:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Prediction file schema invalid for {pred_path}: got {pred_cols}, expected {expected_pred_cols}"
        )

if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["baseline_condition_allowed"] or TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["strict_no_prior_iri"]:
    raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} leaked into feature sets.")
if "IRI_mean_x" not in feature_sets["baseline_condition_allowed"]:
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x missing from baseline_condition_allowed.")
if "IRI_mean_x" in feature_sets["strict_no_prior_iri"]:
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x leaked into strict_no_prior_iri.")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 13) Write required lightweight mm records.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
all_artifacts_saved = (
    [str(p) for p in required_artifacts]
    + trained_model_artifacts
    + prediction_artifacts
    + feature_importance_artifacts
    + all_plot_paths_saved
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="modeling_pipeline_design",
    text="MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "feature_set_names": list(feature_sets.keys()),
        "model_names": successful_model_names,
        "artifacts_saved": all_artifacts_saved,
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="model_metrics",
    text="MODEL_METRICS IRI_mean_y regression model comparison.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "metrics_path": str(model_performance_metrics_path),
        "model_metrics_json_path": str(model_metrics_json_path),
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "best_test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "best_test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "best_test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "artifacts_saved": [str(model_performance_metrics_path), str(model_metrics_json_path), str(model_comparison_report_path)],
    }
)

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="model_metrics_per_model",
        text=f"metrics {row['model_name']} {row['feature_set']} IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "test_rmse": safe_float(row["test_rmse"]),
            "test_mae": safe_float(row["test_mae"]),
            "test_r2": safe_float(row["test_r2"]),
            "model_artifact_path": str(row["model_artifact_path"]),
            "prediction_artifact_path": str(row["prediction_artifact_path"]),
        }
    )

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="trained_model",
        text="Trained model artifact for IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "model_artifact_path": str(row["model_artifact_path"]),
            "metrics_summary": {
                "test_rmse": safe_float(row["test_rmse"]),
                "test_mae": safe_float(row["test_mae"]),
                "test_r2": safe_float(row["test_r2"]),
            },
        }
    )

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    pred_path = str(row["prediction_artifact_path"])
    pred_rows = int(len(pd.read_csv(pred_path)))
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="model_predictions",
        text="Prediction artifact for IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "prediction_artifact_path": pred_path,
            "row_count": pred_rows,
        }
    )

mm.rag_add(
    kind="model_diagnostics",
    text="Model diagnostic plots for IRI_mean_y regression.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "plot_paths": all_plot_paths_saved,
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "artifacts_saved": all_plot_paths_saved + [str(feature_importance_best_models_path)],
    }
)

# Confirm records before pipeline state.
modeling_design_hits = rag_search(
    kind="modeling_pipeline_design",
    query="MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)
model_metrics_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS IRI_mean_y regression model comparison",
    k=10
)
per_model_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=20
)
trained_model_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="trained_model",
    query="Trained model artifact for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=20
)
model_predictions_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_predictions",
    query="Prediction artifact for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=20
)
model_diagnostics_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_diagnostics",
    query="Model diagnostic plots for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)

if not modeling_design_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="modeling_pipeline_design".')
if not model_metrics_hits_after:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="model_metrics".')
if len(per_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="model_metrics_per_model".')
if len(trained_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="trained_model".')
if len(model_predictions_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="model_predictions".')
if not model_diagnostics_hits_after:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="model_diagnostics".')

mm.rag_add(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    text=(
        "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN. "
        "Regression modeling completed for IRI_mean_y with leakage-controlled feature-set comparison."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "artifacts_saved": all_artifacts_saved,
    }
)

pipeline_state_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN",
    k=10
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 14) Final hard gate.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
missing_gate_items = []
if not modeling_design_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="modeling_pipeline_design"')
if not model_metrics_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="model_metrics"')
if len(per_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="model_metrics_per_model" for every run')
if len(trained_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="trained_model" for every run')
if len(model_predictions_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="model_predictions" for every run')
if not model_diagnostics_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="model_diagnostics"')
if not pipeline_state_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm pipeline_state PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN')
if missing_artifacts:
    missing_gate_items.extend(missing_artifacts)
if missing_plots:
    missing_gate_items.extend(missing_plots)
if len(successful_model_names) < 3:
    missing_gate_items.append("fewer than three successful advanced model types")
if set(feature_sets.keys()) != {"baseline_condition_allowed", "strict_no_prior_iri"}:
    missing_gate_items.append("required feature sets missing")
if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["baseline_condition_allowed"] or TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets["strict_no_prior_iri"]:
    missing_gate_items.append("target leaked into feature sets")

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN hard gate failed. Missing/invalid: "
        + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 15) Print reviewer gate evidence.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
gate_report = {
    "status": "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN_COMPLETED_VERIFIED_PENDING_REVIEWER_APPROVAL",
    "DATASET_ID": DATASET_ID,
    "FILE_PATH": FILE_PATH,
    "file_exists": os.path.exists(FILE_PATH),
    "file_path_outside_WORK_DIR": True,
    "PINNED_FILE_PATH_matches_registry": (
        True if not pinned else str(Path(pinned).resolve()) == str(file_path_resolved)
    ),
    "state_already_completed_before_run": state_already_completed_before_run,
    "dataset_shape": [int(df.shape[0]), int(df.shape[1])],
    "modeling_row_count_after_dropping_missing_target": int(df_model.shape[0]),
    "dataset_columns_as_List_str": dataset_columns,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "approved_artifacts_used": {k: str(v) for k, v in approved_artifact_paths.items()},
    "required_memory_retrieval_hit_counts": {
        "target_candidates_hits": len(target_candidates_memory_hits),
        "dataset_artifact_hits_for_COLUMNS_query": len(dataset_artifact_memory_hits),
        "model_metrics_hits_prior_audit_only": len(model_metrics_memory_hits),
        "model_metrics_per_model_hits_prior_audit_only": len(model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits),
        "discover_pipeline_state_hits": len(discover_state_hits),
        "target_select_pipeline_state_hits": len(target_select_state_hits),
        "eda_feature_engineering_pipeline_state_hits": len(eda_state_hits),
    },
    "train_test_split_summary": train_test_split_metadata,
    "cv_strategy": modeling_plan["cv_strategy"],
    "feature_sets": feature_sets,
    "leakage_checks": modeling_feature_sets["leakage_checks"],
    "models_optimized": successful_model_names,
    "model_count_successful": int(len(successful_model_names)),
    "model_run_count_successful": int(len(metrics_df)),
    "skipped_models": skipped_models,
    "metrics_artifacts": {
        "model_performance_metrics_csv": str(model_performance_metrics_path),
        "model_metrics_json": str(model_metrics_json_path),
        "model_comparison_report_md": str(model_comparison_report_path),
    },
    "prediction_artifacts": prediction_artifacts,
    "trained_model_artifacts": trained_model_artifacts,
    "best_model": {
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "best_model_path": str(best_model_path),
        "best_model_metadata_path": str(best_model_metadata_path),
    },
    "best_model_metrics": {
        "test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "test_median_absolute_error": safe_float(best_row["test_median_absolute_error"]),
        "test_explained_variance": safe_float(best_row["test_explained_variance"]),
        "test_smape": safe_float(best_row["test_smape"]),
    },
    "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_summary": iri_sensitivity,
    "feature_importance_artifacts": feature_importance_artifacts,
    "plot_paths": all_plot_paths_saved,
    "mm_record_confirmation": {
        "modeling_pipeline_design_hits": len(modeling_design_hits),
        "model_metrics_hits": len(model_metrics_hits_after),
        "model_metrics_per_model_hits": len(per_model_hits_after),
        "trained_model_hits": len(trained_model_hits_after),
        "model_predictions_hits": len(model_predictions_hits_after),
        "model_diagnostics_hits": len(model_diagnostics_hits_after),
        "pipeline_state_hits": len(pipeline_state_hits_after),
    },
    "pipeline_state_completed_text": "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN",
}

print(json.dumps(gate_report, indent=2))

- MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN is rejected because the reported model metrics/XAI artifacts are not organized under the required `WORK_DIR/metrics/` directory and the saved model-comparison metrics were not reported in the required long machine-readable schema with `model_name`, `metric_name`, `value`, and `timestamp`.

ISSUES:
- `model_performance_metrics.csv` and `model_metrics.json` were reported at `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/`, not under `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/metrics/`.
- Feature-importance/XAI CSV artifacts were reported at `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/`, not under an organized metrics/XAI location.
- The reported `model_performance_metrics.csv` is a wide table and does not satisfy the required machine-readable traceability schema containing at least:
  - `model_name`
  - `metric_name`
  - `value`
  - `timestamp`
- Required output organization was not satisfied for modeling QA:
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/metrics/`
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/models/`
  - `/content/pavement_agentic_workspace/plots/`
- Existing modeling work appears otherwise aligned with the approved target `IRI_mean_y`, required feature-set comparison, required models, leakage controls, prediction artifacts, trained model artifacts, and diagnostic plots, so coder should repair artifact organization/schema rather than retrain unless necessary.

  ```text
