STATE_TASK: MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN

```python
from mm_runtime import mm
mm.health_check()

import os
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pandas as pd

WORK_DIR = "/content/pavement_agentic_workspace"
STATE_NOW = "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN"
TARGET_COLUMN = "IRI_mean_y"
TASK_TYPE = "regression"

# Registry is the ONLY source of dataset path.
reg = mm.validate_registry(WORK_DIR)
FILE_PATH = reg["latest_path"]
DATASET_ID = reg["latest_dataset_id"]

if not os.path.exists(FILE_PATH):
    raise FileNotFoundError(FILE_PATH)

work_dir = Path(WORK_DIR)
work_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

work_dir_resolved = work_dir.resolve()
file_path_resolved = Path(FILE_PATH).resolve()
try:
    file_path_resolved.relative_to(work_dir_resolved)
    raise RuntimeError(f"Registry FILE_PATH must not be inside WORK_DIR: {FILE_PATH}")
except ValueError:
    pass

pinned = os.environ.get("PINNED_FILE_PATH")
if pinned and str(Path(pinned).resolve()) != str(file_path_resolved):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "PINNED_FILE_PATH does not match registry latest_path. "
        f"PINNED_FILE_PATH={pinned}; registry latest_path={FILE_PATH}"
    )

base_meta = {
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
}

metrics_dir = work_dir / "metrics"
models_dir = work_dir / "models"
plots_dir = work_dir / "plots"
predictions_dir = work_dir / "predictions"

metrics_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
models_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
plots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
predictions_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

# Existing/root artifacts from prior modeling run.
root_modeling_plan_path = work_dir / "modeling_plan.json"
root_modeling_feature_sets_path = work_dir / "modeling_feature_sets.json"
root_split_metadata_path = work_dir / "train_test_split_metadata.json"
root_metrics_csv_path = work_dir / "model_performance_metrics.csv"
root_model_metrics_json_path = work_dir / "model_metrics.json"
root_report_path = work_dir / "model_comparison_report.md"
root_feature_importance_best_path = work_dir / "feature_importance_best_models.csv"

# Organized metrics artifacts required by reviewer.
metrics_csv_path = metrics_dir / "model_performance_metrics.csv"
metrics_json_path = metrics_dir / "model_metrics.json"
metrics_long_path = metrics_dir / "model_metrics_long.csv"
metrics_feature_importance_best_path = metrics_dir / "feature_importance_best_models.csv"

best_model_path = models_dir / "best_model.joblib"
best_model_metadata_path = models_dir / "best_model_metadata.json"

required_wide_columns = [
    "dataset_id",
    "feature_set",
    "model_name",
    "cv_rmse_mean",
    "cv_rmse_std",
    "cv_mae_mean",
    "cv_r2_mean",
    "test_rmse",
    "test_mae",
    "test_r2",
    "test_median_absolute_error",
    "test_explained_variance",
    "test_smape",
    "best_params",
    "model_artifact_path",
    "prediction_artifact_path",
]

required_long_schema = [
    "dataset_id",
    "file_path",
    "state",
    "feature_set",
    "model_name",
    "metric_name",
    "value",
    "timestamp",
]

metric_names_for_long = [
    "cv_rmse_mean",
    "cv_rmse_std",
    "cv_mae_mean",
    "cv_r2_mean",
    "test_rmse",
    "test_mae",
    "test_r2",
    "test_median_absolute_error",
    "test_explained_variance",
    "test_smape",
]

required_prediction_schema = [
    "row_id",
    "feature_set",
    "model_name",
    "y_true",
    "y_pred",
    "residual",
    "absolute_error",
    "squared_error",
]

required_plot_names = [
    "model_comparison_test_rmse.png",
    "model_comparison_test_mae.png",
    "model_comparison_test_r2.png",
    "cv_rmse_by_model.png",
    "observed_vs_predicted_best_model.png",
    "residuals_vs_predicted_best_model.png",
    "residual_distribution_best_model.png",
    "absolute_error_by_feature_set.png",
    "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison.png",
    "feature_importance_best_model.png",
]

def rag_search(kind, query, k=10):
    return mm.rag_search(
        query=query,
        kind=kind,
        k=k,
        meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
    )

def safe_float(x):
    try:
        if pd.isna(x):
            return None
        return float(x)
    except Exception:
        return None

def copy_if_exists(src, dst):
    src = Path(src)
    dst = Path(dst)
    if not src.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Required source artifact missing: {src}")
    if src.resolve() != dst.resolve():
        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
    return str(dst)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1) Required memory retrievals and upstream state confirmations.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
target_candidates_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="target_candidates",
    query="target_candidates.csv",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
dataset_artifact_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(
    kind="dataset_artifact",
    query="COLUMNS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
model_metrics_memory_hits_prior = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS",
    k=3,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)
model_metrics_per_model_memory_hits_prior = mm.rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics",
    k=10,
    meta_filters={"dataset_id": DATASET_ID}
)

discover_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET",
    k=10
)
target_select_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT",
    k=10
)
eda_state_hits = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN",
    k=10
)
modeling_state_hits_before = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN",
    k=10
)

missing_upstream = []
if not discover_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET")
if not target_select_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT")
if not eda_state_hits:
    missing_upstream.append("PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN")
if missing_upstream:
    raise RuntimeError("Missing upstream pipeline states: " + "; ".join(missing_upstream))

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2) Verify existing modeling outputs needed for repair.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
required_existing_files = [
    root_modeling_plan_path,
    root_modeling_feature_sets_path,
    root_split_metadata_path,
    root_metrics_csv_path,
    root_report_path,
    best_model_path,
    best_model_metadata_path,
]
for p in required_existing_files:
    if not p.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Cannot repair modeling organization because required artifact is missing: {p}")

metrics_df = pd.read_csv(root_metrics_csv_path)
missing_wide_cols = [c for c in required_wide_columns if c not in metrics_df.columns]
if missing_wide_cols:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Existing model_performance_metrics.csv missing required wide columns: "
        + "; ".join(missing_wide_cols)
    )

if metrics_df.empty:
    raise RuntimeError("Existing model_performance_metrics.csv is empty.")

feature_sets_seen = sorted(metrics_df["feature_set"].astype(str).unique().tolist())
model_names_seen = sorted(metrics_df["model_name"].astype(str).unique().tolist())
if set(feature_sets_seen) != {"baseline_condition_allowed", "strict_no_prior_iri"}:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Required feature sets not both present in metrics: {feature_sets_seen}")
if len(model_names_seen) < 3:
    raise RuntimeError(f"Fewer than three model types present in metrics: {model_names_seen}")

# Validate modeling feature sets leakage rules.
with open(root_modeling_feature_sets_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    modeling_feature_sets = json.load(f)

feature_sets = modeling_feature_sets.get("feature_sets", {})
if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets.get("baseline_condition_allowed", []):
    raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} leaked into baseline_condition_allowed")
if TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets.get("strict_no_prior_iri", []):
    raise RuntimeError(f"{TARGET_COLUMN} leaked into strict_no_prior_iri")
if "IRI_mean_x" not in feature_sets.get("baseline_condition_allowed", []):
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x must be included in baseline_condition_allowed")
if "IRI_mean_x" in feature_sets.get("strict_no_prior_iri", []):
    raise RuntimeError("IRI_mean_x must be excluded from strict_no_prior_iri")

# Validate model/prediction artifact paths listed in metrics.
model_artifacts = []
prediction_artifacts = []
for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    model_path = Path(str(row["model_artifact_path"]))
    pred_path = Path(str(row["prediction_artifact_path"]))
    if not model_path.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Model artifact listed in metrics does not exist: {model_path}")
    if models_dir.resolve() not in model_path.resolve().parents and model_path.resolve() != models_dir.resolve():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Model artifact must be under {models_dir}: {model_path}")
    if not pred_path.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Prediction artifact listed in metrics does not exist: {pred_path}")
    if predictions_dir.resolve() not in pred_path.resolve().parents and pred_path.resolve() != predictions_dir.resolve():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Prediction artifact must be under {predictions_dir}: {pred_path}")
    pred_cols = list(pd.read_csv(pred_path, nrows=1).columns)
    if pred_cols != required_prediction_schema:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Prediction schema invalid for {pred_path}. "
            f"Got {pred_cols}; expected {required_prediction_schema}"
        )
    model_artifacts.append(str(model_path))
    prediction_artifacts.append(str(pred_path))

# Validate plots.
required_plot_paths = [plots_dir / name for name in required_plot_names]
missing_plots = [str(p) for p in required_plot_paths if not p.exists()]
if missing_plots:
    raise RuntimeError("Missing required modeling plots: " + "; ".join(missing_plots))

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3) Create organized /metrics artifacts without retraining.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
copy_if_exists(root_metrics_csv_path, metrics_csv_path)

# Per-model/native and best feature-importance artifacts moved/copied into metrics dir.
feature_importance_metric_paths = []
for fi_src in sorted(work_dir.glob("feature_importance*.csv")):
    fi_dst = metrics_dir / fi_src.name
    copy_if_exists(fi_src, fi_dst)
    feature_importance_metric_paths.append(str(fi_dst))

if not metrics_feature_importance_best_path.exists():
    if root_feature_importance_best_path.exists():
        copy_if_exists(root_feature_importance_best_path, metrics_feature_importance_best_path)
        if str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path) not in feature_importance_metric_paths:
            feature_importance_metric_paths.append(str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path))
    else:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Required feature importance artifact missing: {root_feature_importance_best_path}")

# Create required long-form machine-readable metrics audit table.
timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
long_rows = []
for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    for metric_name in metric_names_for_long:
        long_rows.append({
            "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
            "file_path": FILE_PATH,
            "state": STATE_NOW,
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "metric_name": metric_name,
            "value": safe_float(row[metric_name]),
            "timestamp": timestamp,
        })

long_df = pd.DataFrame(long_rows, columns=required_long_schema)
if list(long_df.columns) != required_long_schema:
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"model_metrics_long.csv internal schema invalid: {list(long_df.columns)}"
    )
if len(long_df) != len(metrics_df) * len(metric_names_for_long):
    raise RuntimeError(
        "model_metrics_long.csv row count invalid. "
        f"Got {len(long_df)}, expected {len(metrics_df) * len(metric_names_for_long)}"
    )
long_df.to_csv(metrics_long_path, index=False)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4) Update root and organized JSON/report references.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
best_row = metrics_df.sort_values(["test_rmse", "test_mae"]).iloc[0].to_dict()
best_model_name = str(best_row["model_name"])
best_feature_set = str(best_row["feature_set"])

best_by_feature_set = {}
for fs in feature_sets_seen:
    sub = metrics_df[metrics_df["feature_set"].astype(str) == fs].sort_values(["test_rmse", "test_mae"])
    best_by_feature_set[fs] = sub.iloc[0].to_dict()

base_best = best_by_feature_set["baseline_condition_allowed"]
strict_best = best_by_feature_set["strict_no_prior_iri"]
iri_sensitivity = {
    "baseline_condition_allowed_best_model": str(base_best["model_name"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_best_model": str(strict_best["model_name"]),
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_rmse": safe_float(base_best["test_rmse"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_rmse": safe_float(strict_best["test_rmse"]),
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_mae": safe_float(base_best["test_mae"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_mae": safe_float(strict_best["test_mae"]),
    "baseline_condition_allowed_test_r2": safe_float(base_best["test_r2"]),
    "strict_no_prior_iri_test_r2": safe_float(strict_best["test_r2"]),
    "delta_RMSE_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_rmse"] - base_best["test_rmse"]),
    "delta_MAE_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_mae"] - base_best["test_mae"]),
    "delta_R2_strict_minus_baseline": safe_float(strict_best["test_r2"] - base_best["test_r2"]),
    "interpretation": (
        "A performance drop when IRI_mean_x is removed does not automatically prove leakage, "
        "but indicates dependence on prior IRI and requires deployment-time availability confirmation."
    ),
}

if root_model_metrics_json_path.exists():
    with open(root_model_metrics_json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        model_metrics_json = json.load(f)
else:
    model_metrics_json = {}

model_metrics_json.update({
    "dataset_id": DATASET_ID,
    "file_path": FILE_PATH,
    "state": STATE_NOW,
    "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
    "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
    "primary_selection_metric": "lowest test_rmse",
    "metrics_path": str(metrics_csv_path),
    "model_metrics_json_path": str(metrics_json_path),
    "model_metrics_long_path": str(metrics_long_path),
    "feature_importance_best_models_path": str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path),
    "organized_metrics_directory": str(metrics_dir),
    "models_directory": str(models_dir),
    "plots_directory": str(plots_dir),
    "all_results": metrics_df[required_wide_columns].to_dict(orient="records"),
    "best_model": {
        "feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "model_name": best_model_name,
        "test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "model_artifact_path": str(best_model_path),
        "source_model_artifact_path": str(best_row["model_artifact_path"]),
        "prediction_artifact_path": str(best_row["prediction_artifact_path"]),
    },
    "best_by_feature_set": {
        fs: {
            "feature_set": fs,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "test_rmse": safe_float(row["test_rmse"]),
            "test_mae": safe_float(row["test_mae"]),
            "test_r2": safe_float(row["test_r2"]),
            "model_artifact_path": str(row["model_artifact_path"]),
            "prediction_artifact_path": str(row["prediction_artifact_path"]),
        }
        for fs, row in best_by_feature_set.items()
    },
    "iri_mean_x_sensitivity_summary": iri_sensitivity,
    "artifact_organization": {
        "metrics_dir": str(metrics_dir),
        "models_dir": str(models_dir),
        "plots_dir": str(plots_dir),
        "predictions_dir": str(predictions_dir),
        "metrics_artifacts": [
            str(metrics_csv_path),
            str(metrics_json_path),
            str(metrics_long_path),
            str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path),
        ] + feature_importance_metric_paths,
    },
})

with open(root_model_metrics_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(model_metrics_json, f, indent=2)
with open(metrics_json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(model_metrics_json, f, indent=2)

# Update modeling_plan.json with organized artifact paths.
with open(root_modeling_plan_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    modeling_plan = json.load(f)

modeling_plan["artifact_organization"] = {
    "metrics_dir": str(metrics_dir),
    "models_dir": str(models_dir),
    "plots_dir": str(plots_dir),
    "predictions_dir": str(predictions_dir),
}
modeling_plan["metrics_artifacts"] = {
    "model_performance_metrics_csv": str(metrics_csv_path),
    "model_metrics_json": str(metrics_json_path),
    "model_metrics_long_csv": str(metrics_long_path),
    "feature_importance_best_models_csv": str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path),
    "per_model_feature_importance_csvs": feature_importance_metric_paths,
}
modeling_plan["repair_note"] = (
    "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN artifact organization repaired after reviewer request. "
    "No retraining performed; existing split, models, predictions, and plots preserved."
)
with open(root_modeling_plan_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    json.dump(modeling_plan, f, indent=2)

# Update report with repair section.
report_append = f"""

---

## Reviewer Repair: Organized Metrics/XAI Artifacts

Repair timestamp UTC: `{timestamp}`

No retraining was performed. Existing target, feature sets, split, predictions, trained model artifacts, and plots were preserved.

Organized artifact directories:

- Metrics/XAI: `{metrics_dir}`
- Models: `{models_dir}`
- Plots: `{plots_dir}`
- Predictions: `{predictions_dir}`

Metrics artifacts now include:

- `{metrics_csv_path}`
- `{metrics_json_path}`
- `{metrics_long_path}`
- `{metrics_feature_importance_best_path}`

`model_metrics_long.csv` audit schema:

```text
{",".join(required_long_schema)}
```

Long metrics row count: `{len(long_df)}`

Best model remains:

```json
{json.dumps(model_metrics_json["best_model"], indent=2)}
```
"""
with open(root_report_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(report_append)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5) Verify repaired artifacts and organization.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
repaired_required_files = [
    metrics_csv_path,
    metrics_json_path,
    metrics_long_path,
    metrics_feature_importance_best_path,
    root_modeling_plan_path,
    root_model_metrics_json_path,
    root_report_path,
    best_model_path,
    best_model_metadata_path,
]
for p in repaired_required_files:
    if not p.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Repaired required artifact missing: {p}")

check_long = pd.read_csv(metrics_long_path)
if list(check_long.columns) != required_long_schema:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Saved model_metrics_long.csv schema invalid. "
        f"Got {list(check_long.columns)}, expected {required_long_schema}"
    )
if len(check_long) < len(metrics_df) * len(metric_names_for_long):
    raise RuntimeError(
        f"Saved model_metrics_long.csv row count too small: {len(check_long)}"
    )

check_metrics = pd.read_csv(metrics_csv_path)
missing_repaired_cols = [c for c in required_wide_columns if c not in check_metrics.columns]
if missing_repaired_cols:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "Repaired metrics/model_performance_metrics.csv missing columns: "
        + "; ".join(missing_repaired_cols)
    )

for p in [metrics_csv_path, metrics_json_path, metrics_long_path, metrics_feature_importance_best_path]:
    if metrics_dir.resolve() not in p.resolve().parents and p.resolve() != metrics_dir.resolve():
        raise RuntimeError(f"Metrics artifact not under metrics dir: {p}")

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6) Write/repair lightweight mm records with organized paths.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
organized_metrics_artifacts = [
    str(metrics_csv_path),
    str(metrics_json_path),
    str(metrics_long_path),
    str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path),
] + feature_importance_metric_paths

organized_model_artifacts = sorted(set(model_artifacts + [str(best_model_path), str(best_model_metadata_path)]))
organized_prediction_artifacts = sorted(set(prediction_artifacts))
organized_plot_artifacts = sorted(str(p) for p in required_plot_paths)

artifacts_saved_all = (
    [str(root_modeling_plan_path), str(root_modeling_feature_sets_path), str(root_split_metadata_path), str(root_report_path)]
    + organized_metrics_artifacts
    + organized_model_artifacts
    + organized_prediction_artifacts
    + organized_plot_artifacts
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="modeling_pipeline_design",
    text=(
        "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression. "
        "Repair organized metrics/XAI artifacts under WORK_DIR/metrics without retraining."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "feature_set_names": feature_sets_seen,
        "model_names": model_names_seen,
        "metrics_dir": str(metrics_dir),
        "models_dir": str(models_dir),
        "plots_dir": str(plots_dir),
        "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved_all,
    }
)

mm.rag_add(
    kind="model_metrics",
    text="MODEL_METRICS IRI_mean_y regression model comparison.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "metrics_path": str(metrics_csv_path),
        "model_metrics_json_path": str(metrics_json_path),
        "model_metrics_long_path": str(metrics_long_path),
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "best_test_rmse": safe_float(best_row["test_rmse"]),
        "best_test_mae": safe_float(best_row["test_mae"]),
        "best_test_r2": safe_float(best_row["test_r2"]),
        "artifacts_saved": organized_metrics_artifacts,
    }
)

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="model_metrics_per_model",
        text=f"metrics {row['model_name']} {row['feature_set']} IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "test_rmse": safe_float(row["test_rmse"]),
            "test_mae": safe_float(row["test_mae"]),
            "test_r2": safe_float(row["test_r2"]),
            "metrics_path": str(metrics_csv_path),
            "model_metrics_long_path": str(metrics_long_path),
            "model_artifact_path": str(row["model_artifact_path"]),
            "prediction_artifact_path": str(row["prediction_artifact_path"]),
        }
    )

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="trained_model",
        text="Trained model artifact for IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "model_artifact_path": str(row["model_artifact_path"]),
            "metrics_summary": {
                "test_rmse": safe_float(row["test_rmse"]),
                "test_mae": safe_float(row["test_mae"]),
                "test_r2": safe_float(row["test_r2"]),
            },
        }
    )

for _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():
    pred_path = str(row["prediction_artifact_path"])
    pred_rows = int(len(pd.read_csv(pred_path)))
    mm.rag_add(
        kind="model_predictions",
        text="Prediction artifact for IRI_mean_y regression.",
        meta={
            **base_meta,
            "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
            "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
            "model_name": str(row["model_name"]),
            "feature_set": str(row["feature_set"]),
            "prediction_artifact_path": pred_path,
            "row_count": pred_rows,
        }
    )

mm.rag_add(
    kind="model_diagnostics",
    text="Model diagnostic plots for IRI_mean_y regression.",
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "plot_paths": organized_plot_artifacts,
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "artifacts_saved": organized_plot_artifacts + [str(metrics_feature_importance_best_path)],
    }
)

# Confirm mm records before pipeline state.
modeling_design_hits = rag_search(
    kind="modeling_pipeline_design",
    query="MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN completed for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)
model_metrics_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics",
    query="MODEL_METRICS IRI_mean_y regression model comparison",
    k=10
)
per_model_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_metrics_per_model",
    query="metrics IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=50
)
trained_model_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="trained_model",
    query="Trained model artifact for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=50
)
model_predictions_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_predictions",
    query="Prediction artifact for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=50
)
model_diagnostics_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="model_diagnostics",
    query="Model diagnostic plots for IRI_mean_y regression",
    k=10
)

if not modeling_design_hits:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="modeling_pipeline_design" after repair.')
if not model_metrics_hits_after:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="model_metrics" after repair.')
if len(per_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="model_metrics_per_model" after repair.')
if len(trained_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="trained_model" after repair.')
if len(model_predictions_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm records kind="model_predictions" after repair.')
if not model_diagnostics_hits_after:
    raise RuntimeError('Missing mm record kind="model_diagnostics" after repair.')

mm.rag_add(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    text=(
        "PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN. "
        "Modeling artifacts repaired with organized metrics/XAI directory and long-form metrics audit schema."
    ),
    meta={
        **base_meta,
        "target_column": TARGET_COLUMN,
        "task_type": TASK_TYPE,
        "best_model_name": best_model_name,
        "best_feature_set": best_feature_set,
        "metrics_dir": str(metrics_dir),
        "models_dir": str(models_dir),
        "plots_dir": str(plots_dir),
        "artifacts_saved": artifacts_saved_all,
    }
)

pipeline_state_hits_after = rag_search(
    kind="pipeline_state",
    query="PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN",
    k=10
)

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7) Final hard gate for reviewer-requested repair.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
missing_gate_items = []
for p in repaired_required_files:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(str(p))
for p in required_plot_paths:
    if not p.exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(str(p))
for p in model_artifacts:
    if not Path(p).exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(p)
for p in prediction_artifacts:
    if not Path(p).exists():
        missing_gate_items.append(p)
if list(check_long.columns) != required_long_schema:
    missing_gate_items.append("model_metrics_long.csv exact schema")
if len(check_long) < len(metrics_df) * len(metric_names_for_long):
    missing_gate_items.append("model_metrics_long.csv row count")
if not modeling_design_hits:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="modeling_pipeline_design"')
if not model_metrics_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="model_metrics"')
if len(per_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="model_metrics_per_model"')
if len(trained_model_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="trained_model"')
if len(model_predictions_hits_after) < len(metrics_df):
    missing_gate_items.append('mm records kind="model_predictions"')
if not model_diagnostics_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append('mm record kind="model_diagnostics"')
if not pipeline_state_hits_after:
    missing_gate_items.append("PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN")

if missing_gate_items:
    raise RuntimeError(
        "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN repair hard gate failed. Missing/invalid: "
        + "; ".join(missing_gate_items)
    )

# -------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8) Print reviewer gate evidence.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------
df_head = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False, nrows=5)
dataset_columns = [str(c) for c in df_head.columns.tolist()]
# Full shape without retaining full dataframe in memory longer than needed.
full_shape = pd.read_csv(FILE_PATH, low_memory=False).shape

gate_report = {
    "status": "MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN_REPAIRED_VERIFIED_PENDING_REVIEWER_APPROVAL",
    "repair_scope": (

    "prediction_artifacts_under_predictions_dir": organized_prediction_artifacts,
    "plots_under_plots_dir": organized_plot_artifacts,
    "model_metrics_long_csv_schema": list(check_long.columns),
    "model_metrics_long_csv_required_schema": required_long_schema,
    "model_metrics_long_csv_row_count": int(len(check_long)),
    "model_metrics_long_metric_names": sorted(check_long["metric_name"].astype(str).unique().tolist()),
    "wide_metrics_csv_columns": list(check_metrics.columns),
    "feature_sets": feature_sets,
    "leakage_checks": {
        "IRI_mean_y_excluded_from_all_feature_sets": (
            TARGET_COLUMN not in feature_sets.get("baseline_condition_allowed", [])
            and TARGET_COLUMN not in feature_sets.get("strict_no_prior_iri", [])
        ),
        "IRI_mean_x_included_only_in_baseline_condition_allowed": (
            "IRI_mean_x" in feature_sets.get("baseline_condition_allowed", [])
            and "IRI_mean_x" not in feature_sets.get("strict_no_prior_iri", [])

    "models_optimized_preserved": model_names_seen,
    "model_run_count_preserved": int(len(metrics_df)),

\\"model_name\\",\\n    \\"metric_name\\",\\n    \\"value\\",\\n    \\"timestamp\\",\\n]\\n\\nmetric_names_for_long = [\\n    \\"cv_rmse_mean\\",\\n    \\"cv_rmse_std\\",\\n    \\"cv_mae_mean\\",\\n    \\"cv_r2_mean\\",\\n    \\"test_rmse\\",\\n    \\"test_mae\\",\\n    \\"test_r2\\",\\n    \\"test_median_absolute_error\\",\\n    \\"test_explained_variance\\",\\n    \\"test_smape\\",\\n]\\n\\nrequired_prediction_schema = [\\n    \\"row_id\\",\\n    \\"feature_set\\",\\n    \\"model_name\\",\\n    \\"y_true\\",\\n    \\"y_pred\\",\\n    \\"residual\\",\\n    \\"absolute_error\\",\\n    \\"squared_error\\",\\n]\\n\\nrequired_plot_names = [\\n    \\"model_comparison_test_rmse.png\\",\\n    \\"model_comparison_test_mae.png\\",\\n    \\"model_comparison_test_r2.png\\",\\n    \\"cv_rmse_by_model.png\\",\\n    \\"observed_vs_predicted_best_model.png\\",\\n    \\"residuals_vs_predicted_best_model.png\\",\\n    \\"residual_distribution_best_model.png\\",\\n    \\"absolute_error_by_feature_set.png\\",\\n    \\"iri_mean_x_sensitivity_comparison.png\\",\\n    \\"feature_importance_best_model.png\\",\\n]\\n\\ndef rag_search(kind, query, k=10):\\n    return mm.rag_search(\\n        query=query,\\n        kind=kind,\\n        k=k,\\n        meta_filters={\\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID}\\n    )\\n\\ndef safe_float(x):\\n    try:\\n        if pd.isna(x):\\n            return None\\n        return float(x)\\n    except Exception:\\n        return None\\n\\ndef json_clean(obj):\\n    if isinstance(obj, dict):\\n        return {str(k): json_clean(v) for k, v in obj.items()}\\n    if isinstance(obj, list):\\n        return [json_clean(v) for v in obj]\\n    if isinstance(obj, tuple):\\n        return [json_clean(v) for v in obj]\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.integer,)):\\n        return int(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.floating,)):\\n        return None if pd.isna(obj) else float(obj)\\n    if isinstance(obj, (np.bool_,)):\\n        return bool(obj)\\n    try:\\n        if pd.isna(obj):\\n            return None\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    return obj\\n\\ndef copy_if_exists(src, dst):\\n    src = Path(src)\\n    dst = Path(dst)\\n    if not src.exists():\\n        raise RuntimeError(f\\"Required source artifact missing: {src}\\")\\n    if src.resolve() != dst.resolve():\\n        shutil.copy2(src, dst)\\n    return str(dst)\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 1) Required memory retrievals and upstream state confirmations.\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\ntarget_candidates_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"target_candidates\\",\\n    query=\\"target_candidates.csv\\",\\n    k=3,\\n    meta_filters={\\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID}\\n)\\ndataset_artifact_memory_hits = mm.rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"dataset_artifact\\",\\n    query=\\"COLUMNS\\",\\n    k=3,\\n    meta_filters={\\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID}\\n)\\nmodel_metrics_memory_hits_prior = mm.rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"model_metrics\\",\\n    query=\\"MODEL_METRICS\\",\\n    k=3,\\n    meta_filters={\\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID}\\n)\\nmodel_metrics_per_model_memory_hits_prior = mm.rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"model_metrics_per_model\\",\\n    query=\\"metrics\\",\\n    k=10,\\n    meta_filters={\\"dataset_id\\": DATASET_ID}\\n)\\n\\ndiscover_state_hits = rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"pipeline_state\\",\\n    query=\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET\\",\\n    k=10\\n)\\ntarget_select_state_hits = rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"pipeline_state\\",\\n    query=\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT\\",\\n    k=10\\n)\\neda_state_hits = rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"pipeline_state\\",\\n    query=\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN\\",\\n    k=10\\n)\\nmodeling_state_hits_before = rag_search(\\n    kind=\\"pipeline_state\\",\\n    query=\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=MODELING_PIPELINE_DESIGN\\",\\n    k=10\\n)\\n\\nmissing_upstream = []\\nif not discover_state_hits:\\n    missing_upstream.append(\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=DISCOVER_DATASET\\")\\nif not target_select_state_hits:\\n    missing_upstream.append(\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=TARGET_SELECT\\")\\nif not eda_state_hits:\\n    missing_upstream.append(\\"PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=EDA_FEATURE_ENGINEERING_DESIGN\\")\\nif missing_upstream:\\n    raise RuntimeError(\\"Missing upstream pipeline states: \\" + \\"; \\".join(missing_upstream))\\n\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# 2) Verify existing modeling outputs needed for repair.\\n# -------------------------------------------------------------------\\nrequired_existing_files = [\\n    root_modeling_plan_path,\\n    root_modeling_feature_sets_path,\\n    root_split_metadata_path,\\n    root_metrics_csv_path,\\n    root_report_path,\\n    best_model_path,\\n    best_model_metadata_path,\\n]\\nfor p in required_existing_files:\\n    if not p.exists():\\n        raise RuntimeError(f\\"Cannot repair modeling organization because required artifact is missing: {p}\\")\\n\\nmetrics_df = pd.read_csv(root_metrics_csv_path)\\nmissing_wide_cols = [c for c in required_wide_columns if c not in metrics_df.columns]\\nif missing_wide_cols:\\n    raise RuntimeError(\\n        \\"Existing model_performance_metrics.csv missing required wide columns: \\"\\n        + \\"; \\".join(missing_wide_cols)\\n    )\\n\\nif metrics_df.empty:\\n    raise RuntimeError(\\"Existing model_performance_metrics.csv is empty.\\")\\n\\nfeature_sets_seen = sorted(metrics_df[\\"feature_set\\"].astype(str).unique().tolist())\\nmodel_names_seen = sorted(metrics_df[\\"model_name\\"].astype(str).unique().tolist())\\nif set(feature_sets_seen) != {\\"baseline_condition_allowed\\", \\"strict_no_prior_iri\\"}:\\n    raise RuntimeError(f\\"Required feature sets not both present in metrics: {feature_sets_seen}\\")\\nif len(model_names_seen) < TARGET_COLUMN in feature_sets.get(\\"baseline_condition_allowed\\", []):\\n    raise RuntimeError(f\\"{TARGET_COLUMN} leaked into baseline_condition_allowed\\")\\nif TARGET_COLUMN in metrics.\\nmodel_artifacts = []\\nprediction_artifacts = []\\nfor _, row in metrics_df.iterrows():\\n    model_path = Path(str(row[\\"model_artifact_path\\"]))\\n    pred_path = Path(str(row[\\"prediction_artifact_path\\"]))\\n    if not model_path.exists():\\n        raise RuntimeError(f\\"Model artifact listed in metrics does not exist: {model_path}\\")\\n    if {model_path}\\")\\n    if not pred_path.exists():\\n        raise RuntimeError(f\\"Prediction artifact listed in metrics does not exist: {pred_path}\\")\\n    if predictions_dir.resolve() not in = list(pd.read_csv(pred_path, nrows=1).columns)\\n    if pred_cols != required_prediction_schema:\\n        raise RuntimeError(\\n            f\\"Prediction schema invalid for {pred_path}. \\"\\n            f\\"Got {pred_cols}; expected {required_prediction_schema}\\"\\n        )\\n    model_artifacts.append(str(model_path))\\n    prediction_artifacts.append(str(pred_path))\\n\\n# Validate \\"baseline_condition_allowed_best_model\\": str(base_best[\\"model_name\\"]),\\n    \\"strict_no_prior_iri_best_model\\": str(strict_best[\\"model_name\\"]),\\n    \\"baseline_condition_allowed_test_rmse\\": safe_float(base_best[\\"test_rmse\\"]),\\n    \\"strict_no_prior_iri_test_rmse\\": safe_float(strict_best[\\"test_rmse\\"]),\\n    \\"baseline_condition_allowed_test_mae\\": safe_float(base_best[\\"test_mae\\"]),\\n    \\"strict_no_prior_iri_test_mae\\": safe_float(strict_best[\\"test_mae\\"]),\\n ## DOMAIN_INTERPRETATION — Pavement Engineering Interpretation

No code was run and no artifacts were created. The following interpretation is strictly from a pavement engineering and transportation asset management perspective, using the approved modeling evidence provided for the `IRI_mean_y` regression task.

---

# 1. Engineering meaning of the target: `IRI_mean_y`

`IRI_mean_y` represents the modeled pavement condition outcome, interpreted as the later, post-period, or response-side International Roughness Index.

From the observed value range previously reported:

- Minimum: about `30`
- Mean: about `87`
- Median: about `72`
- Maximum: about `388`

the units are most likely **inches per mile**, which is common for U.S. pavement management and HPMS/LTPP-style datasets.

From an engineering standpoint:

- Lower IRI means smoother pavement and better ride quality.
- Higher IRI means rougher pavement, worse user comfort, higher vehicle operating costs, and often greater maintenance need.
- IRI is not a complete structural condition measure, but it is one of the most widely used network-level pavement performance indicators.

Typical roughness interpretation, depending on agency thresholds:

| IRI, in/mi | General interpretation |
|---:|---|
| `< 95` | Good / smooth |
| `95–170` | Fair / moderate roughness |
| `> 170` | Poor / rough |

Thresholds vary by road class, speed, and agency policy, so these should be calibrated to the owner-agency’s pavement management rules.

---

# 2. Interpretation of approved best model accuracy

Approved best model:

```text
HistGradientBoostingRegressor
Feature set: baseline_condition_allowed
Target: IRI_mean_y
```

Approved metrics:

| Metric | Value | Pavement engineering interpretation |
|---|---:|---|
| RMSE | `21.9044` | Typical larger-error-sensitive prediction error is about 22 IRI units, likely in/mi. |
| MAE | `14.5319` | Average absolute error is about 15 IRI units. |
| Median absolute error | `8.9006` | For at least half the sections, error is under about 9 IRI units. |
| R² | `0.7957` | Model explains about 80% of variation in outcome IRI. |
| Explained variance | `0.7961` | Consistent with strong explanatory performance. |
| SMAPE | `16.0909%` | Relative error is moderate and generally usable for network-level screening. |

## Engineering meaning

An RMSE of about **22 in/mi** and MAE of about **15 in/mi** are reasonably strong for a network-level pavement condition prediction problem, especially with mixed traffic, structure, climate, condition, and treatment variables.

However, these errors must be interpreted relative to decision thresholds. For example:

- If an agency’s good/fair boundary is near `95 in/mi`, a ±15 to ±22 in/mi error can move some sections across condition categories.
- The model is likely more reliable for **network-level planning, prioritization, and risk screening** than for final project-level treatment design.
- The median absolute error of about `8.9` suggests many predictions are close, but RMSE being much larger indicates some high-error sections or outliers.

Practical implication:

> The model appears useful for pavement management forecasting and prioritization, but predicted IRI should be treated as a probabilistic planning estimate, not as a substitute for field measurement or engineering judgment.

---

# 3. `IRI_mean_x` dependence: engineering plausibility and leakage concern

The approved sensitivity results are important:

| Feature set | Best model | RMSE | MAE | R² |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| `baseline_condition_allowed` | HistGradientBoostingRegressor | `21.9044` | `14.5319` | `0.7957` |
| `strict_no_prior_iri` | HistGradientBoostingRegressor | `34.1715` | `23.5992` | `0.5029` |

Difference when removing `IRI_mean_x`:

```text
ΔRMSE strict-minus-baseline = +12.2671
ΔMAE strict-minus-baseline  = +9.0672
ΔR² strict-minus-baseline   = -0.2928
```

## Engineering interpretation

The model performs much better when `IRI_mean_x` is included. This is **engineering-plausible** because pavement roughness is highly persistent over time.

A pavement section that is already rough tends to remain rough or deteriorate further unless a treatment improves ride quality. Similarly, a smooth section often remains smoother for some time depending on structure, traffic, climate, age, and maintenance history.

So, from a pavement performance perspective:

```text
IRI at time x is expected to be one of the strongest predictors of IRI at time y.
```

The large performance drop when excluding `IRI_mean_x` does not automatically prove leakage. It may simply mean the model is using a legitimate baseline condition measurement.

## Leakage concern

The concern is temporal provenance.

`IRI_mean_x` is acceptable only if it was measured **before** `IRI_mean_y` and before the prediction decision point.

It becomes leakage if:

- `IRI_mean_x` and `IRI_mean_y` are from the same measurement window,
- `IRI_mean_x` was measured after treatment or after the target period,
- the `_x` / `_y` merge does not represent a true before/after temporal relationship,
- `IRI_mean_x` was derived from or averaged with the same observations used in `IRI_mean_y`,
- any delta/improvement/deterioration variables were implicitly used.

## Deployment implication

The approved best model depends materially on prior IRI. Therefore:

- If baseline IRI is available at deployment time, the best model is practically valuable.
- If baseline IRI is not available, stale, or measured inconsistently, the strict no-prior-IRI performance is more realistic.
- The strict model’s RMSE of about `34.2` and R² of about `0.50` indicate that traffic, structure, climate, age, treatment, and other distress variables still carry useful information, but with substantially less predictive power.

Recommended interpretation:

> The baseline-condition model is a strong “condition-updating” model. The strict no-prior-IRI model is a more conservative planning model when current IRI is unavailable or temporally uncertain.

---

# 4. Engineering relevance of feature groups

## 4.1 Traffic / loading variables

Relevant columns:

```text
AADT_mean_x
AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x
AADT_Combination_mean_x
Future_AADT_mean_x
```

Engineering meaning:

- `AADT_mean_x`: average annual daily traffic, vehicles/day.
- `AADT_Single_Unit_mean_x`: single-unit truck traffic, vehicles/day.
- `AADT_Combination_mean_x`: combination truck traffic, vehicles/day.
- `Future_AADT_mean_x`: likely forecast traffic volume.

In pavement engineering, traffic loading is a primary driver of deterioration. Truck traffic is especially important because pavement damage is nonlinear with axle load. Combination trucks often contribute disproportionately to structural damage compared with passenger vehicles.

Interpretation cautions:

- AADT alone is a volume measure, not a direct load measure.
- Equivalent Single Axle Loads, axle spectra, or truck load factors would be more mechanistic if available.
- `Future_AADT_mean_x` should be treated as availability-sensitive. It is not automatically leakage if it is a forecast available at planning time, but it should not be based on information unavailable at prediction time.

Asset-management relevance:

- Supports deterioration forecasting under traffic growth.
- Helps prioritize heavily loaded corridors.
- Useful for scenario planning if forecast traffic is reliable.

---

## 4.2 Pavement structure variables

Relevant columns:

```text
Thickness_Rigid_mean_x
Thickness_Flexible_mean_x
Base_Thickness_mean_x
Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x
Surface_Type_mode
Base_Type_mode_x
```

Engineering meaning:

- `Thickness_Rigid_mean_x`: concrete or rigid pavement thickness, likely inches.
- `Thickness_Flexible_mean_x`: asphalt/flexible layer thickness, likely inches.
- `Base_Thickness_mean_x`: aggregate or stabilized base thickness, likely inches.
- `Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x`: thickness of most recent overlay, likely inches.
- `Surface_Type_mode`: dominant surface type.
- `Base_Type_mode_x`: dominant base type.

Engineering relevance:

- Thicker or better-designed pavement structures usually deteriorate more slowly.
- Surface and base type affect moisture sensitivity, stiffness, fatigue, rutting, and roughness progression.
- Overlay thickness is especially important because resurfacing can reset or improve IRI.

Cautions:

- Layer thickness alone does not describe structural capacity fully.
- Materials, modulus, drainage, construction quality, subgrade, and maintenance history also matter.
- `Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x` has dual interpretation: structure variable and treatment-history variable.

Asset-management relevance:

- Useful for treatment selection and expected service life.
- Helps distinguish structurally weak sections from rough but structurally adequate sections.

---

## 4.3 Baseline condition variables

Relevant columns:

```text
IRI_mean_x
Rutting_mean_x
Cracking_Percent_mean_x
Faulting_mean_x
```

Engineering meaning:

- `IRI_mean_x`: baseline/current roughness before the target period.
- `Rutting_mean_x`: wheel-path rut depth, likely inches.
- `Cracking_Percent_mean_x`: percent of pavement area or lane affected by cracking.
- `Faulting_mean_x`: slab joint faulting, likely inches, primarily relevant to concrete pavements.

Engineering relevance:

- Baseline condition variables are among the most important pavement performance predictors.
- Roughness, rutting, cracking, and faulting are different distress modes but often interact.
- Cracking can lead to water infiltration, base weakening, and roughness progression.
- Rutting affects ride quality and safety.
- Faulting directly contributes to roughness on jointed concrete pavements.

Cautions:

- These variables must be measured before `IRI_mean_y`.
- If measured after treatment, they can become post-outcome leakage.
- `IRI_mean_x` should remain explicitly governed because of its strong predictive influence.

Asset-management relevance:

- Supports deterioration modeling.
- Supports treatment trigger logic.
- Helps prioritize sections with multiple distress modes.

---

## 4.4 Treatment variables

Relevant columns:

```text
Treatment_type
Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x
```

Engineering meaning:

- `Treatment_type`: maintenance or rehabilitation action category.
- `Last_Overlay_Thickness_mean_x`: overlay thickness, also a proxy for treatment intensity.

Engineering relevance:

Treatment type is central to pavement performance because different treatments have different expected effects:

- Thin overlay: improves ride and seals surface, moderate service-life extension.
- Thick overlay/rehabilitation: greater structural and ride improvement.
- Preventive maintenance: may preserve condition but not dramatically reduce IRI.
- Reconstruction: major reset of condition, if present.

Cautions:

- Treatment variables are often subject to selection bias. Agencies treat worse roads differently from better roads.
- If treatment was applied after the target measurement, it would be leakage.
- Treatment timing relative to `IRI_mean_y` must be confirmed.

Asset-management relevance:

- Useful for estimating treatment effectiveness.
- Supports life-cycle planning.
- Can inform expected post-treatment IRI or deterioration trajectories.

---

## 4.5 Climate / environment variables

Relevant columns:

```text
RHU_AV_x
FRZ_IDX_x
TEMP_AVG_x
PRECIPITATION_x
```

Engineering meaning:

- `RHU_AV_x`: average relative humidity, likely percent.
- `FRZ_IDX_x`: freezing index, likely degree-days or equivalent freeze severity measure.
- `TEMP_AVG_x`: average temperature.
- `PRECIPITATION_x`: precipitation amount.

Engineering relevance:

Climate is a major pavement deterioration driver:

- Freeze-thaw cycles can damage base/subgrade and accelerate roughness.
- Precipitation and moisture can weaken unbound layers and subgrade.
- High temperatures can accelerate asphalt rutting and oxidation.
- Temperature variation contributes to thermal cracking and slab movement.

Asset-management relevance:

- Useful for regional calibration.
- Supports climate-resilience planning.
- Helps identify environmentally vulnerable corridors.

Cautions:

- Climate variables are often spatially coarse.
- Local drainage, groundwater, and subgrade are often more explanatory but may be absent.
- Climate effects may interact with structure and traffic.

---

## 4.6 Age / system / context variables

Relevant columns:

```text
Age_x
F_System_mode
Urban_Type
```

Engineering meaning:

- `Age_x`: pavement age or age since construction/treatment, likely years.
- `F_System_mode`: functional system classification.
- `Urban_Type`: urban/rural or urbanization category.

Engineering relevance:

- Pavement age is a key deterioration proxy.
- Functional class affects design standard, traffic type, speed, and maintenance priority.
- Urban/rural context affects traffic loading, drainage, utility cuts, speed environment, and maintenance practices.

Asset-management relevance:

- Useful for network segmentation.
- Helps compare similar facilities.
- Supports policy-level prioritization.

Cautions:

- Age definition must be clarified: age since construction, last major rehab, last overlay, or data year?
- Functional class can encode agency management practices, not just engineering behavior.

---

# 5. Asset-management use cases

The approved model can support several pavement management activities.

## 5.1 Network-level condition forecasting

The model can estimate likely future/post-period IRI for many segments, helping agencies anticipate future condition distributions.

Useful for:

- annual network condition forecasts,
- performance reporting,
- identifying sections likely to cross roughness thresholds.

## 5.2 Treatment prioritization support

Predicted IRI can help rank candidate sections for maintenance or rehabilitation, especially when combined with distress and traffic.

Use cautiously:

- Predicted IRI should not be the only prioritization criterion.
- Safety, structural capacity, equity, funding constraints, and route importance should also be considered.

## 5.3 Budget and scenario planning

The model can support scenario-level questions such as:

- Which sections may become poor within the next cycle?
- How does forecast traffic growth affect roughness?
- Which climates or systems show higher risk?

## 5.4 Performance management

Predictions can support agency targets such as percent lane-miles in good/fair/poor condition.

Caution:

- Because RMSE is around 22 in/mi, condition-category forecasts near threshold boundaries should include uncertainty bands.

---

# 6. Deployment cautions

## 6.1 Confirm temporal ordering

Before operational deployment, the agency must confirm:

```text
IRI_mean_x measured before IRI_mean_y
baseline distresses measured before IRI_mean_y
treatment variables refer to treatments known before prediction
Future_AADT available at prediction/planning time
```

Without this, the strong performance of the baseline-condition model may be partly inflated.

## 6.2 Maintain both approved feature-set views

Both feature sets remain valuable:

### `baseline_condition_allowed`

Best for:

- annual pavement management updates,
- cases where current measured IRI is available,
- short- to medium-term condition forecasting.

Risk:

- temporal leakage if `IRI_mean_x` is not truly prior.

### `strict_no_prior_iri`

Best for:

- early planning when current IRI is unavailable,
- deployment environments with uncertain condition data timing,
- sensitivity benchmark.

Risk:

- lower accuracy and weaker explanatory power.

## 6.3 Avoid overinterpreting model output as causal

The model is predictive, not necessarily causal.

For example:

- A treatment type may appear associated with higher IRI because worse roads were selected for that treatment.
- High traffic roads may have better condition if they receive more frequent maintenance.
- Functional system may reflect agency funding priority as much as physical deterioration.

Therefore, model outputs should inform decisions but not replace engineering causal analysis.

---

# 7. Governance recommendations for future validation

## 7.1 Temporal validation

Use a true time-based validation if possible:

- train on earlier years,
- test on later years,
- ensure no segment has future information in predictors.

This is the most important next validation for leakage control.

## 7.2 Spatial or route-based validation

Evaluate performance by:

- district,
- county,
- route,
- climate zone,
- urban/rural class,
- functional system.

This checks whether the model generalizes across the network.

## 7.3 Segment-level independence review

If multiple records come from the same route or segment family, random train/test splitting may overstate performance. A grouped split by roadway section, route, or management segment would better test deployment realism.

## 7.4 Threshold-based error analysis

In pavement management, classification around thresholds matters. Evaluate prediction errors near agency-specific IRI thresholds, such as:

- good/fair boundary,
- fair/poor boundary,
- treatment trigger levels.

Sections close to thresholds should be flagged as uncertain.

## 7.5 Residual review by engineering categories

Review whether errors are systematically higher for:

- very high IRI sections,
- recently treated sections,
- high truck corridors,
- rigid pavement sections with faulting,
- freeze-thaw regions,
- specific treatment types,
- urban roads.

This helps detect bias and missing engineering variables.

---

# 8. Overall pavement-engineering conclusion

The approved modeling results are technically plausible and useful for transportation asset management.

The best model using the `baseline_condition_allowed` feature set achieved:

```text
RMSE ≈ 21.9
MAE ≈ 14.5
R² ≈ 0.80
```

This is strong network-level predictive performance for future or post-period IRI.

However, the sharp performance reduction when excluding `IRI_mean_x` is the central governance issue:

```text
RMSE worsens from 21.9 to 34.2
R² drops from 0.796 to 0.503
```

From an engineering standpoint, this dependence is plausible because roughness persists over time. From a deployment standpoint, it requires strict confirmation that `IRI_mean_x` is a true baseline measurement available before the prediction decision.

Recommended operational stance:

> Use the baseline-condition model when current/pre-outcome IRI is verified available. Use the strict no-prior-IRI model as the conservative benchmark when IRI timing is uncertain. Always report the sensitivity between the two when supporting pavement management decisions.

14. Final report includes pavement-engineering interpretation.
15. Required mm records exist:
   - `final_report`
   - `artifact_manifest`
   - `deployment_readiness`
   - `final_pipeline_summary`
   - `pipeline_state` with `PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=FINAL_REPORTING_ARCHIVE`

```

Expected final pipeline state completion text:

```text
PIPELINE_STATE_COMPLETED=FINAL_REPORTING_ARCHIVE
```

# 9. Final design decision

The final archive should package the approved pavement prediction pipeline as:

```text
Target: IRI_mean_y
Task: regression
Best model: HistGradientBoostingRegressor
Best feature set: baseline_condition_allowed
Best RMSE: 21.9044
Primary deployment caution: verify IRI_mean_x is truly baseline/pre-outcome before using the best model operationally.
```

This final state should not change any modeling outputs. It should only consolidate, document, verify, and make the approved pipeline auditable.
